{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}}
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.(\eset{x_\ell}{y} 
    \semi \eset{\mf{flag}}{\mf{true}})}{\\
      &\quad\ \llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}}
  }
}\\
\mhl{\llrr{e}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}}
&= (\mf{flag}, \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^\mf{Succ})\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Succ}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Succ}}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Succ}}
&= \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Succ}}\\
\llrr{e}^\mf{Succ} &= \llrr{e}
\end{align*}

Example:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{
  &\elet{x}{w * w}{\\
    &\estate{y}{\ell_0}{x}{\\
      &\elet{z}{y * y}{\\
          &\eret{\vtag{[]}{\vtxt{z}}}
      }
    }
  }
}\\
&\qquad=\\
&\elet{\mf{flag}}{\eref{false}}{\\
  &\elet{x}{w * w}{\\
    &\elet{y_\ell}{\eref{x}}{\\
      &\elet{y}{\eget{y_\ell}}{\\
        &\elet{y_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.(\eset{x_\ell}{y} 
    \semi \eset{\mf{flag}}{\mf{true}})}{\\
          &\elet{z}{y * y}{\\
            &(\mf{flag}, \lambda ().(\\
              &\quad\elet{x}{w * w}{\\
                &\quad\elet{y}{\eget{y_\ell}}{\\
                  &\quad\elet{z}{y * y}{\\
                    &\quad\eret{\vtag{[]}{\vtxt{z}}}\\
                  }
                }
              }
            &))
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
\end{align*}

Programs:

This approach does \emph{not} allow for 
mutual recursion between components;
forbidding this capability from the source seems much easier
than adding it to the translation/target.
(e.g. \emph{morally} we want to move all the components 
to a let-rec-and block,
but this breaks FGCBV and messes with simulation correspondence.)
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{P}}^P &= \elet{x}{e}{\llrr{P}^P}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\elet{\C(x)}{\hat{e}}{P}}^P} &=
\elet{x_\C}{\lambda x.\llrr{\hat{e}}}{\llrr{P}^P}\\
\llrr{e}^P &= \llrr{e}
\end{align*}

\subsection{Runtime and Context}

The above translation does not let us reason about the
\emph{intermediate stages} of a program's evaluation,
which is necessary to prove a theorem of the form:
$$\mf{if}\ m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'
\mf{, then}\ \llrr{m} \semi \llrr{e} \mapsto^* \llrr{e'} \semi \llrr{m'}.$$

For instance, how do we translate the (intermediate-only)
value $\vset{\ell}{p}$?

Consider rule \ts{EB-StI}, which says:
\begin{align*}
\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}&}{\mf{Init}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m'}
&&\ts{EB-StI}\\
(m' &= m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v)
\end{align*}
Putting aside the issue of translating $m$,
and ignoring the rerender flag (which causes more problems!)
for brevity, we translate
the left-hand expression as follows (for some $\hat{e}_0$):
\begin{align*}
&\elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  &\elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.\eset{x_\ell}{y}}{\\
      &\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}
    }
  }
}
\end{align*}
To step this expression, by the semantics of the target language,
$\eref{v}$ generates a \emph{fresh} name
(call it $r$ to distinguish it from source-language labels $\ell$),
and puts $v$ on the heap at $r$.
We get the following:
$$\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}
[r/x_\ell, v/x, \lambda y.\eset{r}{y}/x_\mf{set}]$$
which we want to say is equivalent to 
$\llrr{\hat{e}[v/x,\vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}^\mf{Init}_{\hat{e}_0}$.

Now clearly, we need to draw \emph{some} kind of correspondence between
$\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}$ and $\lambda y.\eset{r}{y}$.
\emph{Morally}, the source location $(p, \ell)$
agrees with the target location $r$,
\emph{but since $r$ is freshly generated,
we have no information linking the two.}
There is no way of translating 
$\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}=\lambda y.\eset{r}{y}$
without some additional \emph{context}.

We have a similar problem in translating the following rule:
\begin{align*}
\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}&}{\mf{Succ}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v'/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m}
&&\ts{EB-StS}\\
(v' &= m[p].\mf{st}[\ell])
\end{align*}
In this case, we don't even know how to translate the left-hand body:
\begin{itemize}
\item Our ``context-free'' translation 
$\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^\mf{Succ}
= \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^\mf{Succ}}$
doesn't work, because it leaves $x_\ell$ free,
but in order for this term to step, $x_\ell$ must be bound to
a concrete reference $r$.
\item Furthermore, we don't know \emph{which} such $r$ to use!
Even if we had a map from $(p, \ell) \mapsto r$,
we would \emph{also} need to parameterize our translation
by $p$, since this is given by the operational context
and not the term syntax.
\end{itemize}

\section{RTC-Deltaless}

Removing $\Delta$ (and thus mutual component recursion)
from the source language.

Primary goal: bring source and target languages closer together
by allowing components to be interpreted as let-bound functions,
and avoiding mutual recursion. Gets rid of ``program'' top-level,
whose encoding is not straightforward
and which distracts from the point of useState/components.

Secondary benefits:
\begin{itemize}
\item ``anonymous components'' allow for new expressive patterns
\item should be strongly normalizing
(although this might be hard to prove...)
\end{itemize}

\subsection{Syntax}

Here, $\C$ is a \emph{syntactic token} (like $\lambda$)
instead of a name like before.
\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s 
\mid \lambda x.e \mid \mhl{\C(x).\hat{e}}
\mid [] \mid v :: v\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v}
\mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v}\\
&\quad\mid \mgrey{\pair{v}{v}} \mid \mgrey{\vset{\ell}{p}}\\
\text{Expression } e &::= \eret{v} \mid \elet{x}{e}{e} \mid v\ v \\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}\\
\text{Body }\hat{e} &::= e \mid \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}} \mid \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \arrow{E} \ptp
\mid [\ptp] \\
&\quad\mid \tview \mid \tattr
\mid \mhl{\tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}} \mid \tset{\ptp}\\
\text{Effect } E &::= \epsilon \mid \emut\\
\text{Label-context }\Ell &::= \map{\ell}{\ptp}
\end{align*}

\subsection{Typing}

\subsubsection{Value Typing}

Judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \Gamma[x] = \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash x: \ptp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.e: \ptp_1 \xrightarrow{E} \ptp_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \C(x).\hat{e} : \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash []: [\ptp]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: [\ptp]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1 :: v_2: [\ptp]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Text] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vtxt{v}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Tag] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: [\tattr]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: [\tview]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vtag{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Attr] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tstr
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vattr{v_1}{v_2}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Click] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tone \arrow{E} \tone
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vclick{v}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\grey{TV-Spec}] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \pair{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\grey{TV-Set}] {
  \Pi[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\ptp}{\Ell}
  \and
  \ell \in \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \Ell[\ell]
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Expression Typing}

Judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid E$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp_1
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppC] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \tview \mid \epsilon
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tset{\ptp}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \tone \mid \emut
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\ptp_1]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_2 \mid E
  \\
  \Gamma, x_\mf{hd}: \ptp_1, x_\mf{tl}: [\ptp_1] \semi \Pi
  \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Body Typing}

Judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
  \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsection{Runtime}

\subsubsection{Syntax}

\begin{align*}
\text{Runtime values } u
&::= x \mid () \mid [] \mid u :: u
\mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{u}
\mid p\\
\text{Runtime expressions } \rho
&::= \eret{u} \mid \elet{x}{\rho}{\rho}\\
&\quad\mid \einit{v} \mid \einits{v} \mid \escan{v} \mid \escans{v}\\
&\quad\mid \echk{u} \mid \echks{u}
\mid \esearch{id}{u} \mid \esearches{id}{u}\\
&\quad\mid \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}} \mid \enew{\phi}{p}{u}
\mid \ehdl{e} \mid \eprog{e}\\
\text{Runtime type } \rtp &::= \tone \mid \ttree
\mid [\ttree]
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Typing}

Values:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Var] {
  \Gamma[x] = \rtp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash x: \rtp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash []: [\ttree]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Lit] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash \elit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u_1: \ttree
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u_2: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u_1 :: u_2: [\ttree]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Node] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\tattr]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash \enode{id}{v}{u}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Path] {
  \Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash p: \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

Expressions:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Ret] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \rtp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eret{u}: \rtp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \rho_1: \rtp_1
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \rtp_1 \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \rho_2: \rtp_2
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \elet{x}{\rho_1}{\rho_2}: \rtp_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tview
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \einit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Inits] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\tview]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \einits{v}: [\ttree]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Scan] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tview
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \escan{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Scans] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\tview]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \escans{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Chk] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \echk{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Chks] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \echks{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Sr] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \esearch{id}{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Srs] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \esearches{id}{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \Pi[p]: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell_0}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell_0 = \Ell \cup \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \Pi[p]: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell_0}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \Ell_0
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-NewI] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \Pi[p]: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell_0}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
  \and
  \Ell_0 = \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{u}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-NewS] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \Pi[p]: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell_0}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
  \and
  \Ell_0 = \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{u}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Hdl] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tone \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \ehdl{e}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Prog] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eprog{e}: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{FGCBV Semantics}

Admin:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Let] {
  m \semi \rho_1 \hookrightarrow \rho_1' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \elet{x}{\rho_1}{\rho_2}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\rho_1'}{\rho_2} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \elet{x}{\eret{u}}{\rho}
&\hookrightarrow
\rho[u/x] \semi m
&&\ts{ER-Ret}\\
\end{align*}

Initialization:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ITag] {
  x\ \mf{fresh}
  \and
  \mf{id}\ \mf{fresh}
} {
  m \semi \einit{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\einits{v_2}}{\eret{\enode{id}{v_1}{x}}} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ISpec] {
  p\ \mf{fresh}
  \\
  m'=m[p \mapsto \{
    \mf{spec}=\pair{\C(x).\hat{e}}{v}, 
    \mf{st}: \cdot, 
    \mf{tree}: \cdot, 
    \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}
  \}]
} {
  m \semi \einit{\pair{\C(x).\hat{e}}{v}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \einit{\vtxt{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{\elit{v}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ILit}\\
m \semi \einits{[]}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{[]} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-INil}\\
m \semi \einits{v :: vs}
&\hookrightarrow
(\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\\&\quad
  \elet{xs}{\einits{vs}}{&&\ts{ER-ICons}\\&\quad
    \eret{x :: xs}
  }
}) \semi m
\end{align*}

Component Evaluation:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}
} {
  m \semi \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \eeval{\phi}{p}{\eret{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\enew{\phi}{p}{x}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-New}\\
m \semi \enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{u}
&\hookrightarrow
p \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{tree} = u
&&\ts{ER-NewI}\\
m \semi \enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{u}
&\hookrightarrow
() \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{tree} = u
&&\ts{ER-NewS}\\
\end{align*}

Event Handling:
\begin{align*}
m \semi \esearch{id}{\elit{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SLit}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{p}
&\hookrightarrow
\esearch{id}{m[p].\mf{tree}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SPath}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\enode{id'}{v}{u}}
&\hookrightarrow
\esearches{id}{u} \semi m\quad (\mf{id} \neq \mf{id}')
&&\ts{ER-SNodeF}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\enode{id}{v}{u}}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\escans{v}}{\esearches{id}{u}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SNodeT}\\
m \semi \esearches{id}{[]}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SNil}\\
m \semi \esearches{id}{u :: us}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\esearch{id}{u}}{\esearch{id}{us}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SCons}\\
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \escan{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScAttr}\\
m \semi \escan{\vclick{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\ehdl{v\ ()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScClick}\\
m \semi \escans{[]}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScNil}\\
m \semi \escans{u :: us}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\escan{u}}{\escans{us}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScCons}\\
\end{align*}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Hdl] {
  \estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}
} {
  m \semi \ehdl{e} \hookrightarrow \ehdl{e'} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \ehdl{\eret{()}} 
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-HDone}\\
\end{align*}

Checking:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkPF] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
} {
  m \semi \echk{p}
  \hookrightarrow
  \echk{m[p].\mf{tree}} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkPT] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt
  \and
  m[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\C(x).\hat{e}}{v}
} {
  m \semi \echk{p}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}
  \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \echk{\elit{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ChkLit}\\
m \semi \echk{\enode{id}{v}{u}}
&\hookrightarrow
\echks{u} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ChkNode}\\
m \semi \echks{[]} &\hookrightarrow \eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ChkNil}\\
m \semi \echks{u :: us}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\echk{u}}{\echks{us}}
&&\ts{ER-ChkCons}
\end{align*}

Programs:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Prog] {
  \cdot \semi e \mapsto e' \semi \cdot
} {
  \cdot \semi \eprog{e} \mapsto \eprog{e'} \semi \cdot
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-PDone] {
  m' = m[p_0 \mapsto \{\mf{spec}: \pair{\C(\_).\eret{v}}{()},
  \mf{st}: \cdot,
  \mf{tree}: \cdot, \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}\}]
} {
  m \semi \eprog{\eret{v}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p_0}{x}}
  \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}

Top-Level:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Top] {
  m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow \rho' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow_0 \rho' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \eret{()} 
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\esearch{id}{p_0}}{\echk{p_0}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-Event}
\end{align*}

\section{Reference-Abstract Translation}

\subsection{Interpretations}

We define our translation with respect to a series of
\emph{reference interpretation functions},
which let us map between memory locations
in the source and target languages.

Each such function is a \emph{partial} map
from some domain in the source language
to the set of locations in the target language.

In particular, we have four such functions
(where $P$, $L$, and $R$ are the domains
of paths, labels, and target-langauge references,
respectively):
\begin{itemize}
\item $\mathbb{S} : P \rightharpoonup R$ maps paths to ``specs''
(of type $\tref{\tone \rightarrow \tview}$),
\item $\mathbb{T}  : P \rightharpoonup R$ maps paths to trees
(of type $\tref{\ttree}$),
\item $\mathbb{B}  : P \rightharpoonup R$ maps paths to rerender flags
(of type $\tref{\tbool}$), and
\item $\mathbb{L} : P \times L \rightharpoonup R$ maps path/label pairs
to the corresponding state cell (of varying type).
\end{itemize}

Call the four-tuple
$\I=\four{\mathbb{S}}{\mathbb{T}}{\mathbb{L}}{\mathbb{B}}$
an \emph{interpretation}. We implicitly parametrize all of our
translations by an interpretation.

Define $\Pi \vDash \mathbb{B}$ to hold when
$\mathbb{B}(p)$ is well-defined iff $p \in \dom(\Pi)$.

Define $\Pi \vDash \mathbb{L}$ to hold when
$\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)$ is well-defined iff $\ell \in \Pi[p].\mf{labs}$.

Define $\Pi \vDash \mathbb{S}$ to hold when
$\mathbb{S}(p)$ is well-defined iff $\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt$.

Define $\Pi \vDash \mathbb{T}$ to hold when
$\mathbb{T}(p)$ is well-defined iff $\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt$.

Finally, define $\Pi \vDash \I$ to hold when
$\Pi \vDash \mathbb{L}$, $\Pi \vDash \mathbb{B}$,
$\Pi \vDash \mathbb{S}$, and $\Pi \vDash \mathbb{T}$.

\subsection{Translation}

Types (user-level and runtime):
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\tone} &= \tone\\
\llrr{\tstr} &= \tstr\\
\llrr{A \xrightarrow{E} A} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \llrr{A}\\
\llrr{[A]} &= [\llrr{A}]\\
\llrr{\tview} &= \tview\\
\llrr{\tattr} &= \tattr\\
\mhl{\llrr{\tcomp{A\ \Ell}}} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tview\\
\mhl{\llrr{\tset{A}}} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone\\
\llrr{\ttree} &= \ttree
\end{align*}
Values:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{x} &= x\\
\llrr{()} &= ()\\
\llrr{s} &= s\\
\llrr{\lambda x.e} &= \lambda x.\llrr{e}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\C(x).\hat{e}}} &= \lambda x.\mf{spec}(
  \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}}
)\\
\llrr{[]} &= []\\
\llrr{v_1 :: v_2} &= \llrr{v_1} :: \llrr{v_2}\\
\llrr{\vtxt{v}} &= \vtxt{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}} &= \vtag{\llrr{v_1}}{\llrr{v_2}}\\
\llrr{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}} &= \vattr{\llrr{v_1}}{\llrr{v_2}}\\
\llrr{\vclick{v}} &= \vclick{\llrr{v}}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\pair{v_1}{v_2}}} 
&= \mf{spec}\ \lambda().\llrr{v_1} \llrr{v_2}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}}
&= \lambda x. \eset{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)}{x}
\semi \eset{\mathbb{B}(p)}{\mf{true}}
\end{align*}
Expressions:

In the source language, we have three different ways
to type the same syntax (i.e. we are \emph{not syntax-directed}):
the left-hand side of application
($v_1\ v_2$) can be a lambda, setter, or component.

Component application has substantially different semantics
from function and setter application,
so to distinguish between them
in the translation, we assume an \emph{elaboration pass}
that inserts $@^\C$ in between application operands
(e.g. $v @^\C v$) where
the left-hand operand is (typed as) a component.

(It would be equivalent, but more verbose,
to define a type-directed translation.)
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\eret{v}} &= \eret{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}} &= \elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}}\\
\llrr{v_1\ v_2} &= \llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}\\
\llrr{v_1 @^\C v_2} &= (\lambda x.\eret{
  \mf{spec}\ \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x
}) \llrr{v_2}\\
&(x\ \mf{fresh})\\
\llrr{\ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}}
&= \ecase{\llrr{v}}{[]}{\llrr{e_1}}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{\llrr{e_2}}
\end{align*}

Bodies:

Intuitively, component bodies have two different interpretations:
under $\phi=\mf{Init}$, $\mf{useState}$ \emph{initializes}
a reference, and under $\phi=\mf{Succ}$, $\mf{useState}$
\emph{accesses} a reference.

To model this in a safe way, the translation of $\phi=\mf{Init}$
\emph{returns} the translation of $\phi=\mf{Succ}$,
such that the latter can \emph{close over} the locations
initialized by the former.

A lot of machinery is necessary to achieve this in a way
such that we can reason about intermediate stages of body evaluation.
For instance, due to the introduction of fresh variables,
we parameterize by a context
$$\delta ::= \emptyset \mid \delta, \ell \mapsto x 
\mid \delta, \mf{flag} \mapsto x$$
and define an auxiliary function as follows:
\[\delta(p, \ell) = \begin{cases}
  x & \text{if}\ \ell \mapsto x \in \delta\\
  \mathbb{L}(p, \ell) & \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\]
\[\delta(p, \mf{flag}) = \begin{cases}
  x & \text{if}\ \mf{flag} \mapsto x \in \delta\\
  \mathbb{B}(p) & \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\]
Intuitively, if a variable is in the range of $\delta$,
it was bound previously in the translation;
if not, then we have some raw location (given by $\I$)
instead.

% lambda-MVU: nondet step rule
% function from (event, state) -> state
% reduction rule to introduce event

% or: await-event primitive

% (can i change runtime terms into functions
% that accept new events to continue?)

\begin{align*}
\mhl{\llrr{\hat{e}}}^\top &= \elet{x_f}{\eref{\mf{false}}}
{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/p}_{\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta}
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  (x_\ell\ \mf{fresh})\quad&\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad(\eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}})
      \\&\quad\ 
    }{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}}
  }
}\\
\mhl{\llrr{e}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta}
&= \eret{(\delta(p, \mf{flag}), 
\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta)}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}\\
\mhl{\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}
&= \elet{x}{\eget{\delta(p, \ell)}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad(\eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y} 
        \semi 
        \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}
      )\\&\quad\
    }}
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}
\\
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta &= \llrr{e}
\end{align*}

Runtime values:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{x} &= x\\
\llrr{()} &= ()\\
\llrr{[]} &= []\\
\llrr{u :: u} &= \llrr{u} :: \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\elit{v}} &= \elit{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\enode{id}{v}{u}} &= \enode{id}{\llrr{v}}{\llrr{u}}\\
\mhl{\llrr{p}} &= \mf{path}(\mathbb{S}(p), \mathbb{T}(p), \mathbb{B}(p))
\end{align*}

Runtime expressions:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\eret{u}}_m &= \eret{\llrr{u}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{\rho_1}{\rho}}_m &= 
\elet{x}{\llrr{\rho_1}_m}{\llrr{\rho_2}_m}\\
\llrr{\einit{v}}_m &= \mf{init}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\einits{v}}_m &= \mf{map}\ \mf{init}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\escan{v}}_m &= \mf{scan}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\escans{v}}_m &= \mf{iter}\ \mf{scan}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\echk{u}}_m &= \mf{check}\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\echks{u}}_m &= \mf{iter}\ \mf{check}\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\esearch{id}{u}}_m &= \mf{search}\ \mf{id}\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\esearches{id}{u}}_m &= \mf{iter}\ (\mf{search}\ \mf{id})\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}}_m
&= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\emptyset}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{
    \llrr{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{\mf{tree}}}
  }
}\\
&(m[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\C(x).\hat{e}_0}{v})\\
\llrr{\eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}}
&= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}}_p}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{
    \llrr{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\mf{tree}}}
  }
}\\
\llrr{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{u}}
&= \eset{\mathbb{T}(p)}{\llrr{u}} \semi \eret{\llrr{p}}\\
\llrr{\enew{\mf{Succ}}{p}{u}}
&= \eset{\mathbb{T}(p)}{\llrr{u}}\\
\llrr{\ehdl{e}} &= \llrr{e}\\
\llrr{\eprog{e}} &= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{e}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{b}{\eref{\mf{false}}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{s}{\eref{\lambda ().\llrr{e}}}{\\
      &\quad\ \elet{t}{\eref{\mf{view}}}{
        \text{\hl{EVENT LOOP}}
      }
    }
  }
}
\end{align*}

Memory:

\begin{align*}\llrr{\pi}^p &= \{\mathbb{B}(p) \mapsto \llrr{\pi.\mf{rerender}}\}
\cup \bigcup_{\ell} \{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{\pi.\mf{st}[\ell]}\}\\
&\quad\cup \begin{cases}
\{\mathbb{S}(p) \mapsto \llrr{\pi.\mf{spec}},
  \mathbb{T}(p) \mapsto \llrr{\pi.\mf{tree}}
\} &\pi.\mf{tree}\ \text{defined}\\
\emptyset &\text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\\
\llrr{m} &= \bigcup_p \llrr{m[p]}^p
\end{align*}

Memory typing:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\Pi} &= \bigcup_p \{\mathbb{B}(p) \mapsto \tbool\}
\cup \bigcup_{\ell \in \pi.\mf{labs}} \{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{\Ell[\ell]}\}\\
&\quad\cup \begin{cases}
\{\mathbb{S}(p) \mapsto \tone \rightarrow \tview,
  \mathbb{T}(p) \mapsto \ttree
\} &\pi.\mf{tree}\ \text{defined}\\
\emptyset &\text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\\
&\qquad(\text{where}\ \Pi[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{A}{\Ell})
\end{align*}

\section{Translation Proofs}

We begin with a couple of auxiliary definitions:

Define (in the source language) $\Ell \subseteq \Ell'$ to hold if,
$\forall \ell \in \dom \Ell$, we have $\Ell[\ell] = \Ell'[\ell]$.
(Type equality is defined in the obvious inductive way.)

Define $\Ell \vDash \delta: \Delta$ to hold when:
\begin{itemize}
\item if $\ell \mapsto x_\ell \in \delta$ and $\ell \in \Ell$,
then $x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{\Ell[\ell]}} \in \Delta$, and
\item if $\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f \in \delta$,
then $x_f: \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta$.
\end{itemize}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, values.]
\label{thm:oldtprezval}
If $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A$,
then\\$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v}: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction over the typing derivation.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with the other type preservation theorems.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Var}: We have \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma[x] = \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash x: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
By (1) and definition of $\llrr{\Gamma}$, we get (2) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}[x]=\llrr{\ptp}$. Since $\llrr{x}=x$, we derive
the conclusion as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {\llrr{\Gamma}[x] = \llrr{\ptp}}
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{x}: \llrr{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Unit}: $\llrr{()}=()$ and $\llrr{\tone}=\tone$,
so the conclusion is easy:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{()}: \llrr{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Str}: $\llrr{s}=s$ and $\llrr{\tstr}=\tstr$,
so the conclusion is easy:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{s}: \llrr{\tstr}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Lam}: We have 
$\llrr{\lambda x.e}=\lambda x.\llrr{e}$
and $\llrr{\ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2} 
= \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}$,
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp_2 \mid E
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.e: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezexp}) on (1),
we get that $\llrr{\Gamma, x: \ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e \llrr{e}: \llrr{\ptp_2}$.
\item By definition, this is equivalent to (2) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e \llrr{e}: \llrr{\ptp_2}$.
\item We can derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \vdash_e \llrr{e}: \llrr{\ptp_2}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \lambda x.\llrr{e}: 
  \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Comp}: We have 
$\llrr{\C(x).e}=\lambda x.\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top$
and $\llrr{\tcomp{A\ \Ell}} 
= \llrr{A} \rightarrow (\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview))$,
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \inferrule*{(1)} {\Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \C(x).\hat{e} : \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis on (1) (Theorem \hl{REF}),
we get that $\llrr{\Gamma, x: \ptp} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}}^\top: 
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.
\item By definition, this is equivalent to (2) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}}^\top: 
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.
\item We can derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \Gamma, x: \llrr{\ptp} \semi \Pi 
    \vdash \llrr{\hat{e}}^\top: 
    \tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top 
  : \ptp \rightarrow (\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview))
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Nil}: $\llrr{[]}=[]$ and $\llrr{[\ptp]}=[\llrr{\ptp}]$,
so the conclusion is easy:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash []: [\ptp]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{[]}: \llrr{[\ptp]}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Cons}: We have 
$\llrr{v :: vs} = \llrr{v} :: \llrr{vs}$ 
and $\llrr{[\ptp]}=[\llrr{\ptp}]$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: [\ptp]}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1 :: v_2: [\ptp]
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on each of (1) and (2),
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: \llrr{\ptp}$
and (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_v \llrr{v_2}: [\llrr{\ptp}]$ respectively.
\item We then derive the conclusion:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: \llrr{\ptp}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v v_2: [\llrr{\ptp}]
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
  \vdash_v \llrr{v_1} :: \llrr{v_2}: [\llrr{\ptp}]
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Cases} \ts{TV-Text}, \ts{TV-Tag},
\ts{TV-Attr}, \ts{TV-Click}: elided for the moment.

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Spec}: We have
$\llrr{\pair{v_1}{v_2}} = \mf{spec}(\lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2})$
and $\llrr{\tview} = \tview$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Spec] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \pair{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1) and (2),
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: 
\llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow (\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview))$
and (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_v \llrr{v_2}: \llrr{A}$, respectively.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows 
(let $B=\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$):
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule* {
    \inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
      \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
        \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
        \llrr{v_1}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow B
      }
      \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
        \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
        \llrr{v_2}: \llrr{A}
      }
    } {
      \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
      \llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}: B
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}: \tone \rightarrow B
    \and
    \ts{\small{TV-Lam}}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \mf{spec}(\lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}) : \tview
  \and
  \ts{\small{TV-Spec}}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Set}: We have
$\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}} = \lambda x. \eset{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)}{x}
\semi \eset{\mathbb{B}(p)}{\mf{true}}$,
$\llrr{\tset{A}} = \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone$,
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Pi[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\ptp'}{\Ell}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\ell \in \Pi[p].\mf{labs}}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \Ell[\ell]
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1), (2), and definition of $\llrr{\Pi}$,
we get (3) that $\mathbb{L}(p, \ell) : \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item We also know, e.g. from (1), that 
(4) $\mathbb{B}(p) : \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
    \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
      \mathbb{L}(p, \ell): \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    \mathbb{L}(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
  \
  \inferrule* { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v x: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  (5)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
  \eset{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)}{x}: \tone
  \quad \ts{\small{TE-Set}}
}
\and
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
    \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
      \mathbb{B}(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    \mathbb{B}(p): \tref{\tbool}
  }
  \
  \inferrule* { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_v \mf{true}: \tbool
  }
} {
  (6)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
  \eset{\mathbb{B}(p)}{\mf{true}}: \tone
  \quad \ts{\small{TE-Set}}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
    (5)
    \and
    (6)
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
    \eset{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)}{x}
    \semi \eset{\mathbb{B}(p)}{\mf{true}} : \tone
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v 
  \lambda x. \eset{\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)}{x}
  \semi \eset{\mathbb{B}(p)}{\mf{true}} : \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
}
\end{mathpar}
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, expressions.]
\label{thm:oldtprezexp}
If $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A \mid E$,
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e \llrr{e}: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction over the typing derivation.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with the other type preservation theorems.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Ret}: We have
$\llrr{\eret{v}} = \eret{\llrr{v}}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezval}) on (1),
we get (2) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v}: \llrr{\ptp}$.
\item The conclusion follows easily:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v}: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e \eret{\llrr{v}}: \llrr{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Let}: We have
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}} = \elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1) and (2),
we get (3) that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e \llrr{e_1}: \llrr{\ptp_1}$
and (4) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e \llrr{e_2}: \llrr{\ptp_2}$, respectively.
\item The conclusion again follows easily:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e \llrr{e_1}: \llrr{\ptp_1}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_e \llrr{e_2}: \llrr{\ptp_2}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}}: \llrr{\ptp_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppL}: We have
$\llrr{v_1\ v_2}=\llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}$,
$\llrr{\ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2}=\llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}$,
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp_1
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezval})
on each of (1) and (2), we get (3) that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: 
\llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}$
and (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_2}:
\llrr{\ptp_1}$, respectively.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: 
    \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_2}:
    \llrr{\ptp_1}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}: \llrr{\ptp_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppC}: We have
$\llrr{v_1 @^\C v_2}=(\lambda x.\eret{
  \mf{spec}\ \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x
}) \llrr{v_2}$,
$\llrr{\tview}=\tview$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppC] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1 @^\C v_2: \tview \mid \epsilon
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezval})
on (1),
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
\llrr{v_1}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow B$ 
(since $\llrr{\tcomp{\ptp\ \Ell}}=\llrr{A} \rightarrow B$),
where $B=\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.
\item Again by inductive hypothesis on (2),
we get (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
\llrr{v_2}: \llrr{A}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[Right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[Right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule*[Right=TV-Spec] {
      \inferrule*[Right=TV-Lam] {
        \inferrule*[Right=TE-App] {
          \inferrule*[Right=(3)] { } {
            \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
            \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow B
          }
          \and
          \inferrule*[Right=TV-Var] { } {
            \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
            \vdash_v x: \llrr{A}
          }
        } {
          \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
          \vdash_e \llrr{v_1}\:x: B
        }
      } {
        \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
        \vdash_v \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x: \tone \rightarrow B
      }
    } {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
      \vdash_v \mf{spec}\ \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x: \tview
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_e \eret{\mf{spec}\ \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x}: \tview
  }
} {
  (5)\quad
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \lambda x.\eret{
    \mf{spec}\ \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x
  }: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  (5)
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    \llrr{v_2}: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e (\lambda x.\eret{
    \mf{spec}\ \lambda ().\llrr{v_1}\:x
  }) \llrr{v_2}: \mf{View}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppS}: We have 
$\llrr{v_1\ v_2}=\llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}$,
$\llrr{\tone}=\tone$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tset{\ptp}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \tone \mid \emut
}   
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezval}) on (1), 
since $\llrr{\tset{\ptp}}=\llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone$,
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
v_1: \llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone$.
\item Again by mutual inductive hypothesis on (2),
we get (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
v_2: \llrr{\ptp}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}: 
    \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v_2}: 
    \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}: \llrr{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Case}: We have
$\llrr{\ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}}
= \ecase{\llrr{v}}{[]}{\llrr{e_1}}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{\llrr{e_2}}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  (1) \and (2) \and (3)
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
where:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\arabic*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\ptp_1]$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_2 \mid E$, and
\item $\Gamma, x_\mf{hd}: \ptp_1, x_\mf{tl}: [\ptp_1] \semi \Pi
  \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E$.
\end{enumerate}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on each of (2) and (3),
we get:
\end{itemize}
\begin{enumerate}[resume,label=(\arabic*)]
\item $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e \llrr{e_1}: \llrr{\ptp_2}$
and
\item $\llrr{\Gamma}, x_\mf{hd}: \llrr{\ptp_1}, 
  x_\mf{tl}: [\llrr{\ptp_1}] \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_e \llrr{e_2}: \llrr{\ptp_2}$.
\end{enumerate}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezval}) on (1), 
we also get (6) that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v}: [\llrr{\ptp_1}]$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion directly:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-CaseL] {
  (6) \and (4) \and (5)
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \ecase{\llrr{v}}
    {[]}{\llrr{e_1}}
    {x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{\llrr{e_2}}
  : \llrr{\ptp_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Sub}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
The conclusion is immediate by inductive hypothesis
on the (more conservative) typing premise.
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, bodies, successor phase.]
\label{thm:oldtprezsucc}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\item $\Ell \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\item for all $\ell \in \dom(\Ell)$, either: 
  \begin{enumerate}[label=(iv-\alph*)]
  \item $\ell \mapsto x_\ell \in \delta$ or
  \item $\ell \in \Pi[p].\mf{labs}$ and
  $\Ell \subseteq \Ell_p$, where $\Pi[p].\mf{spec}=\pair{A_p}{\Ell_p}$,
  \end{enumerate}
\item and either:
  \begin{enumerate}[label=(v-\alph*)]
  \item $\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f \in \delta$ or
  \item $p \in \dom(\Pi)$,
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta: \mf{View}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of \emph{(i)}.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with the other type preservation theorems.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Lift}: We have that
$\llrr{e}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta=\llrr{e}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezexp}) on (1),
we get (2) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e
\llrr{e}: \mf{View}$.
\item We weaken $\llrr{\Gamma}$ with $\Delta$
to arrive at the conclusion.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Let}: We have that
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta
= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(1)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezexp}) on (1),
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
\llrr{e}: \llrr{\ptp}$.
\item By inductive hypothesis on (2)
using premises \emph{(ii)}-\emph{(v)},
we get (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta: \tview$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows,
weakening (3) by $\Delta$:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
    \vdash_e
    \llrr{e}: \llrr{\ptp}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
    \vdash_e
    \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta: \tview
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-State}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x}{\eget{\delta(p, \ell)}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad(\eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y} 
        \semi 
        \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}
      )\\&\quad\
    }}
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}
\end{align*}
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
    \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By premise (\emph{iv}), we have two subcases on $\delta(p, \ell)$:
\begin{itemize}
\item If (\emph{iv-a}) holds, then we have 
$\delta(p, \ell) = x_\ell$.
\item By premise \emph{(iii)}, we know
$x_\ell : \tref{\llrr{\ptp}} \in \Delta$.
\item We conclude (3) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \delta(p, \ell) : \tref{\llrr{\ptp}} \in \Delta
} {
  (3)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_v
  \delta(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{\ptp}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item If instead (\emph{iv-b}) holds, then we have that
$\delta(p, \ell) = \mathbb{L}(p, \ell)$
and $\ell \in \Pi[p].\mf{labs}$. (The latter, alongside
premise \emph{(ii)}, implies that
$\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)$ is well-defined.)
\item Then by definition of $\llrr{\Pi}$, we have 
$\mathbb{L}(p, \ell): \llrr{\Ell_p[\ell]} \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item Since $\Ell, \ell: A \subseteq \Ell_p$, we get that
$\mathbb{L}(p, \ell): \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item We conclude (3) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \delta(p, \ell) : \llrr{\ptp} \in \llrr{\Pi}
} {
  (3)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_v
  \delta(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{\ptp}}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in any case,
premise \emph{(iv)} entails (3) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_v
\delta(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{\ptp}}$.
\item We apply analogous logic to premise \emph{(v)}
to get (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_v
\delta(p, \mf{flag}): \tref{\tbool}$:
\begin{itemize}
\item If (\emph{v-a}) holds, then we have 
$\delta(p, \mf{flag}) = x_f$.
\item By premise \emph{(iii)},
$x_f : \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta$.
\item We conclude (4) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \delta(p, \ell) : \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta
} {
  (4)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_v
  \delta(p, \ell): \tref{\tbool}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item If instead (\emph{v-b}) holds, then we have
$\delta(p, \mf{flag}) = \mathbb{B}(p)$
and $p \in \dom(\Pi)$. By the latter and premise \emph{(ii)},
$\mathbb{B}(p)$ is well-defined.
\item By definition of $\llrr{\Pi}$, we have 
$\mathbb{B}(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item We conclude (4) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \delta(p, \mf{flag}) : \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}
} {
  (4)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_v
  \delta(p, \mf{flag}): \tref{\tbool}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item We perform the following intermediate derivations,
crucially showing (5) and (8):
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Get] {
  (3)
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
  \vdash_e
  \eget{\delta(p, \ell)}: \llrr{\ptp} \quad (5)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  (3)
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, y: \llrr{\ptp} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_v y : \llrr{\ptp}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, y: \llrr{\ptp} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
  \vdash_e
  \eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y}
  : \tone \quad (6)
}
\and
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Set] {
  (4)
  \and
  \inferrule*[Right=TV-True] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_v \mf{true} : \tbool
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
  \vdash_e
  \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}
  : \tone \quad (7)
}
\and
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[Right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
      (6) \and (7)
    } {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, y: \llrr{\ptp} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
      \vdash_e
      \eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y} 
      \semi \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}
      : \tone
      }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_v
    \lambda y. \eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y} 
      \semi \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}
    : \llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
  \vdash_e
  \eret{\lambda y. \eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y} 
    \semi \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}
  }: \llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone \quad (8)
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We apply the inductive hypothesis to (1)
using premises \emph{(ii)}-\emph{(v)}
(noting that premise \emph{(iv)} still holds, 
since $\Ell \subseteq \Ell, \ell: A$).
\item We get (9) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x: \llrr{A}, x_\mf{set}: \llrr{\tset{A}} 
\semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta : \tview$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion, noting
that $\llrr{\tset{\ptp}} = \llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone$:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
  (5)
  \and
  \inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
    (8)
    \and
    (9)
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi}
    \vdash_e
    \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\cdots}}
    {\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}: \tview
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x}{\eget{\delta(p, \ell)}}{\cdots}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}
\
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{corollary}[Type preservation, bodies (successor), complete.]
\label{thm:cprezsucc}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$, and
\item $\dom(\Ell) \subseteq \Pi[p].\mf{labs} = \Ell_p$, 
where $\Pi[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{A_p}{\Ell_p}$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\emptyset: \mf{View}$.
\end{corollary}

\begin{corollary}[Type preservation, bodies (successor), static.]
\label{thm:sprezsucc}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \cdot \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Ell \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\item for all $\ell \in \dom(\Ell)$, $\ell \mapsto x_\ell \in \delta$,
\item and $\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f \in \delta$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \cdot
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta: \mf{View}$.
\end{corollary}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, bodies, initial phase.]
\label{thm:oldtprezinit}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Gamma_0 \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell_0$ 
(with $\Ell \subseteq \Ell_0$ and $\Gamma_0 \subseteq \Gamma$),
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\item $\Ell_0 \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\item for all $\ell \in \dom(\Ell_0) \setminus \dom(\Ell)$, either: 
  \begin{enumerate}[label=(v-\alph*)]
  \item $\ell \mapsto x_\ell \in \delta$ or
  \item $\ell \in \Pi[p].\mf{labs}$ and
  $\Ell_0 = \Ell_p$, where $\Pi[p].\mf{spec}=\pair{A_p}{\Ell_p}$,
  \end{enumerate}
\item and either:
  \begin{enumerate}[label=(vi-\alph*)]
  \item $\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f \in \delta$ or
  \item $p \in \dom(\Pi)$,
  \end{enumerate}
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta: 
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.

\begin{Proof}
Let $B = \tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.

We proceed by induction on the derivation of \emph{(i)}.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with the other type preservation theorems.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Lift}: We have
$\llrr{e}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta
= \eret{(\delta(p, \mf{flag}), 
\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta)}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Since $\Ell=\emptyset$, 
premise \emph{(v)} holds
for all $\ell \in \Ell_0$.
\item Thus, by mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezsucc})
using premises \emph{(ii)}-\emph{(vi)},
we get (1) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma_0}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta: \tview$.
\item Since $\Gamma_0 \subseteq \Gamma$, we can weaken (1) to (2)
that $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta: \tview$.
\item We case-analyze on premise \emph{(vi)} as follows:\\
\hl{TODO: pull this out into a lemma}
\begin{itemize}
\item If (\emph{vi-a}) holds, then we have 
$\delta(p, \mf{flag}) = x_f$.
\item By premise \emph{(iv)},
$x_f : \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta$.
\item We conclude (3) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \delta(p, \ell) : \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta
} {
  (4)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_v
  \delta(p, \ell): \tref{\tbool}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item If instead (\emph{vi-b}) holds, then we have
$\delta(p, \mf{flag}) = \mathbb{B}(p)$
and $p \in \dom(\Pi)$. By the latter and premise \emph{(ii)},
$\mathbb{B}(p)$ is well-defined.
\item By definition of $\llrr{\Pi}$, we have 
$\mathbb{B}(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item We conclude (3) as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \delta(p, \mf{flag}) : \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}
} {
  (4)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_v
  \delta(p, \mf{flag}): \tref{\tbool}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either case, we have (3)
that $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_v
\delta(p, \mf{flag}): \tref{\tbool}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Pair] {
    (3) \and
    \inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {(2)} {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
      \vdash_v \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta:
      \tone \rightarrow \tview
    }
  } {
    \Gamma', \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}
    \vdash_v
    (\mathbb{B}(p), 
    \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta): B
  }
} {
  \Gamma', \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_e
  \eret{(\delta(p,\mf{flag}), 
  \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta)}: B
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-State}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  (x_\ell\ \mf{fresh})\quad&\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad(\eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}})
      \\&\quad\ 
    }{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}}
  }
}
\end{align*}
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
    \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item The mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:oldtprezval})
on (1) gives us (3) that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{v}: \llrr{A}$.
\item The inductive hypothesis on (2), 
using premises \emph{(ii)}-\emph{(vi)},
gives us (4):
$$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{\Ell_0[\ell]}},
x: \llrr{A}, x_\mf{set}: \llrr{\tset{\ptp}} \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}
: B$$
\item Since $\Ell, \ell: \ptp \subseteq \Ell_0$,
we get that $\Ell_0[\ell] = \ptp$.
\item Finally, the flag-typing lemma (\hl{TODO})
gives us (5) that $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
\vdash_v \delta(p, \mf{flag}): \tref{\tbool}$.
\item The conclusion can then be shown via the following
series of derivations:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ref] {(3)} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}
  \vdash_e
  \eref{\llrr{v}}: \tref{\llrr{A}} \quad(6)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Get] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} 
    \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} 
  \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
  \eget{x_\ell}: \llrr{A} \quad(7)
}
\and
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Set] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, \cdots
    \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[Right=TV-Var] { } {
    x: \llrr{A}, \cdots
    \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    y: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, x, y: \llrr{A}
  \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \eset{x_\ell}{y}: \tone \quad(8)
}
\and
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Set] {
  (5)
  \and
  \inferrule*[Right=TV-True] { } {
    \cdots \semi \Pi \vdash_v \mf{true}: \tbool
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, x, y: \llrr{A}
  \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}: \tone \quad(9)
}
\and
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[Right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
      (8) \and (9)
    } {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, x, y: \llrr{A}
      \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
      \eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\delta(p, \mf{flag})}{\mf{true}}:
      \tone
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, x: \llrr{A}
    \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
    \lambda y.\cdots: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, x: \llrr{A}
  \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
  \eret{\lambda y.\cdots}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  \quad(10)
}
\and
\inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
  (6)
  \inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
    (7)
    \quad
    \inferrule*[Right=TE-Let] {
      (10) \and (4) 
    } {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, x: \llrr{A}
      \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
      \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\cdots}}{\cdots}: B
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} 
    \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
    \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\cdots}: B
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
  \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\cdots}
  : B
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Let}: TODO, but easy.
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{corollary}[Type preservation, bodies (initial), complete.]
\label{thm:oldtprezinit-ed}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Gamma_0 \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell_0$ 
(with $\Ell \subseteq \Ell_0$ and $\Gamma_0 \subseteq \Gamma$),
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\item $\dom(\Ell_0) = \dom(\Ell) \cup \Pi[p].\mf{labs}$,
\item $\Ell_0 = \Ell_p$, where $\Pi[p].\mf{spec}=\pair{A_p}{\Ell_p}$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\emptyset: 
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.
\end{corollary}

\begin{corollary}[Type preservation, bodies (initial), static.]
\label{thm:sprezinit}
If $\Gamma \semi \cdot \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \cdot
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/p}_{\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}: \mf{View}$.

\begin{Proof}
We want to apply \thref{thm:oldtprezsucc}:
\begin{itemize}
\item premises \emph{(i)} and \emph{(ii)} 
are both given by our premise;
\item premise \emph{(iii)} holds \hl{TODO: does it?};
\item premise \emph{(iv)} holds by definition 
(where $\Delta=x_f: \tref{\tbool}$),
\item premise \emph{(v)} holds trivially, and
\item premise \emph{(vi-a)} is satisfied.
\end{itemize}
\end{Proof}
\end{corollary}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, bodies, top.]
\label{thm:oldtprezbod}
If $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
then\\$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\top}: 
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, runtime values.]
\label{thm:oldtprezrtv}
If $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash u: A$,
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{u}: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the typing premise.

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Var}: We have $\llrr{x}=x$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Var] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {x: A \in \Gamma}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash_v x: A
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1) and definition of $\llrr{\Gamma}$,
we get $x: \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Gamma}$.
\item The conclusion is straightforward:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  x: \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Gamma}
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v x: \llrr{A}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Path}: We have 
$\llrr{p}=\mf{path}(\mathbb{S}(p), \mathbb{T}(p), \mathbb{B}(p))$,
$\llrr{\ttree}=\ttree$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Path] {
  \inferrule*{(1)} {\Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash p: \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1) and the premise that $\Pi \vDash \I$,
we know that all of $\mathbb{S}(p), \mathbb{T}(p), \mathbb{B}(p)$
are well-defined.
\item Furthermore, by definition of $\llrr{\Pi}$, we have:
\begin{itemize}
\item (2) $\mathbb{S}(p) : \tone \rightarrow \tview \in \llrr{\Pi}$,
\item (3) $\mathbb{T}(p) : \ttree \in \llrr{\Pi}$, and
\item (4) $\mathbb{B}(p) : \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\end{itemize}
\item We can now derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {(2)} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \mathbb{S}(p): \tref{\tone \rightarrow \tview} \quad(5)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {(3)} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \mathbb{T}(p): \tref{\ttree} \quad(6)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {(4)} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \mathbb{B}(p): \tref{\tbool} \quad(7)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Path] {
  (5) \and (6) \and (7)
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \mf{path}(\mathbb{S}(p), \mathbb{T}(p), \mathbb{B}(p)): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Other cases}: Elided for the moment.
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Type preservation, runtime expressions.]
\label{thm:oldtprezrte}
If 
\begin{itemize}
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \rho: A$ and
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$,
\end{itemize}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v \llrr{\rho}_m: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the typing premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalI}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}}_m
&= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\emptyset}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{
    \llrr{\enew{\mf{Init}}{p}{\mf{tree}}}_m
  }
}
\end{align*}
where $m[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\C(x).\hat{e}_0}{v}$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Pi[p].\mf{spec} = \pair{\ptp}{\Ell_0}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {\Ell_0 = \Ell \cup \Pi[p].\mf{labs}}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1) and premise \emph{(ii)}, we have (4) that
$\Gamma, x: A \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0 : \Ell_0$
and (5) that $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A$.
\item By substitution lemma \hl{REF},
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0[v/x] : \Ell_0$.
\item We apply \thref{thm:oldtprezinit-ed}
to (2), (6), and (3) to obtain that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\emptyset:
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.
\item \hl{Term is slightly wrong}:
need to destruct the tuple
\end{itemize}

\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\section{Source Language Changes}

\subsection{Cells}

Drawing inspiration from the translation,
we redefine runtime terms in our calculus
to simplify the memory.

This has the notable benefit of
making the tree structure of the DOM \emph{explicit},
which lets us avoid the (gross) reasoning
about pointer linearity that we did before.

$\hat{e}$-memory version:

\begin{align*}
\text{Runtime values } u
&::= x \mid () \mid [] \mid u :: u
\mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{u}
\mid \mhl{\mf{cell}_p(u)}\\
\text{Runtime expressions } \rho
&::= \eret{u} \mid \elet{x}{\rho}{\rho}\\
&\quad\mid \einit{v} \mid \einits{v} \mid \escan{v} \mid \escans{v}\\
&\quad\mid \echk{u} \mid \echks{u}
\mid \esearch{id}{u} \mid \esearches{id}{u}\\
&\quad\mid \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}}
\mid \ehdl{e} \mid \eprog{e}\\
\pi &::= \{\mf{spec}: \hat{e}, \mf{st}: \map{\ell}{v}, \mf{rerender}: b\}\\
\Pi &::= \map{p}{\{\mf{spec}: \Ell, \mf{labs}: \{\num{\ell}\}, \mf{tree}: b\}}
\end{align*}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Cell] {
  \Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt
  \and
  \Pi[p].\mf{spec} = \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{cell}_p(u): \mf{Tree}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ISpec] {
  p\ \mf{fresh}
  \\
  m'=m[p \mapsto \{
    \mf{spec}: \hat{e}[v/x],
    \mf{st}: \cdot,
    \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}
  \}]
} {
  m \semi \einit{\pair{\C(x).\hat{e}}{v}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]} \semi m'
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-PDone] { } {
  \cdot \semi \eprog{\eret{v}}
  \hookrightarrow \einit{v}
  \semi \cdot
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkCF] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
} {
  m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(u)}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\echk{u}}{\eret{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, x)}} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkCT] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt
  \and
  m[p].\mf{spec} = \hat{e}
} {
  m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(u)}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}}
  \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
}
\end{mathpar}

\begin{align*}
m \semi \eeval{\phi}{p}{\eret{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\eret{\mf{cell}_p(x)}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-New}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\mf{cell}_p(u)}
&\hookrightarrow
\esearch{id}{u} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SCell}
\end{align*}

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Top] {
  m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow \rho' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow_0 \rho' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \eret{u} 
&\hookrightarrow_0
\esearch{id}{u} \semi \echk{u} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-Event}
\end{align*}

$\hat{e}$-cell version:

Motivation: avoid synchronization issues with spec in memory
in simulation proof by embedding the spec in the term instead

\begin{align*}
\text{Runtime values } u
&::= x \mid () \mid [] \mid u :: u
\mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{u}
\mid \mhl{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}\\
\text{Runtime expressions } \rho
&::= \eret{u} \mid \elet{x}{\rho}{\rho}\\
&\quad\mid \einit{v} \mid \einits{v} \mid \escan{v} \mid \escans{v}\\
&\quad\mid \echk{u} \mid \echks{u}
\mid \esearch{id}{u} \mid \esearches{id}{u}\\
&\quad\mid \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e} \Rightarrow \hat{e}}
\mid \ehdl{e} \mid \eprog{e}\\
\pi &::= \{\mf{st}: \map{\ell}{v}, \mf{rerender}: b\}\\
\Pi &::= \map{p}{\{\mf{st}: \Ell, \mf{tree}: b\}}
\end{align*}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Cell] {
  \Pi[p].\mf{tree} = \tt
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell = \Pi[p].\mf{labs}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u): \mf{Tree}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell_0
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell_0 = \Ell \cup \Pi[p].\mf{st}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}
  {\hat{e}_0 \Rightarrow \hat{e}}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell_0
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell \subseteq \Ell_0 = \Pi[p].\mf{st}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}
  {\hat{e}_0 \Rightarrow \hat{e}}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ISpec] {
  p\ \mf{fresh}
  \and
  \hat{e}_0 = \hat{e}[v/x]
  \and
  m'=m[p \mapsto \{
    \mf{st}: \cdot,
    \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}
  \}]
} {
  m \semi \einit{\pair{\C(x).\hat{e}}{v}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}_0 \Rightarrow \hat{e}_0} \semi m'
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-PDone] { } {
  \cdot \semi \eprog{\eret{v}}
  \hookrightarrow \einit{v}
  \semi \cdot
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkCF] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
} {
  m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\echk{u}}{\eret{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, x)}} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkCT] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt
} {
  m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e} \Rightarrow \hat{e}}
  \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}
} {
  m \semi \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}_0 \Rightarrow \hat{e}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}_0 \Rightarrow \hat{e}'} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}

\begin{align*}
m \semi \eeval{\phi}{p}{\hat{e} \Rightarrow \eret{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\eret{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, x)}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-New}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\mf{cell}_p(u)}
&\hookrightarrow
\esearch{id}{u} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SCell}
\end{align*}

\begin{align*}
m \semi \eret{u} 
&\hookrightarrow_0
\esearch{id}{u} \semi \echk{u} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-Event}
\end{align*}

\subsection{Propless}

We remove props from components.

This change is orthogonal to the above; a simplification resulting
from the observation that the component ``argument''
doesn't have any role beyond a standard abstraction,
i.e. we can express $\C(x).\hat{e}$ 
by $\lambda x.\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}$.

This has some other simplifying effects,
e.g. removing the prop type in components
and the ``spec'' intermediate value $\pair{v}{v}$.

Other changes include the introduction of cells from above,
as well as:
\begin{itemize}
\item Removal of the $\mf{new}$ construct,
as we no longer need to update the memory once
initialization of the child tree is complete.
\item The ``idle type'' is now $\ttree$ instead of $\tone$,
since it now makes (and maybe before made?) more sense 
to represent our tree as the result of computation,
instead of having to go through memory.
(We no longer have to deal with constants like $p_0$.)
\end{itemize}

\hl{TODO}: maintaining a syntactic distinction between user-level and runtime
means we also have to distinguish $\Gamma$ and $\Gamma_\rho$;
\emph{do we gain enough from this distinction?}

\subsubsection{Syntax}

User-level:
\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s 
\mid \lambda x.e \mid \mhl{\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}}
\mid [] \mid v :: v\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v}
\mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v} \mid \mgrey{\vset{\ell}{p}}\\
\text{Expression } e &::= \eret{v} \mid \elet{x}{e}{e} \mid v\ v \\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}\\
\text{Body }\hat{e} &::= e \mid \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}} \mid \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \arrow{E} \ptp
\mid [\ptp] \\
&\quad\mid \tview \mid \tattr \mid \tset{\ptp}\\
\text{Effect } E &::= \epsilon \mid \emut\\
\text{Label-record }\Ell &::= \map{\ell}{\ptp}
\end{align*}

Runtime-level:
\begin{align*}
\text{Runtime values } u
&::= x \mid () \mid [] \mid u :: u
\mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{u}
\mid \mhl{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}\\
\text{Runtime expressions } \rho
&::= \eret{u} \mid \elet{x}{\rho}{\rho}\\
&\quad\mid \einit{v} \mid \einits{v} \mid \escan{v} \mid \escans{v}\\
&\quad\mid \echk{u} \mid \echks{u}
\mid \esearch{id}{u} \mid \esearches{id}{u}\\
&\quad\mid \hat{e} \gg^\phi_p \hat{e}
\mid \ehdl{e} \mid \eprog{e}\\
\text{Runtime type } \rtp &::= \tone \mid \ttree \mid [\ttree]
\end{align*}

Other internal representations:
\begin{align*}
\text{Variable }x &\in \mf{Var}\\
\text{Pointer }p &\in \mathbb{N}\\
\text{Label }\ell &\in \mathbb{N}\\
\text{Node identifier }\mf{id} &\in \mf{ID}\\
\text{Boolean flag }b &::= \tt \mid \ff\\
\text{Type context }\Gamma &::= \emptyset \mid \Gamma, x: A\\
\text{Pointer context }\Pi &::= \emptyset \mid \Pi, p: \Ell\\
\text{Phase }\phi &::= \mf{Init} \mid \mf{Succ}\\
\text{State store }\varsigma &::= \emptyset \mid \varsigma, \ell: v\\
\text{Component memory }\pi &::= \{\mf{st}: \varsigma, \mf{rerender}: b\}\\
\text{Memory }m &::= \emptyset \mid m, p: \pi\\
\end{align*}

% mainly motivation, broadly accessible
% can assume a little more ST/picalc knowledge, but not much
  % Rast knowledge will be new— start with example?

% only key ideas from our work

% programmer burden is critical
% get rid of two
% condense "inferring refinements" somehow— too much detail

% show a (coarse) example, then done

% three-icon thing?

% above: simon, below: me

% mirror in refinement sec

\subsubsection{Typing}

Value judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  x: \ptp \in \Gamma
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash x: \ptp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.e: \ptp_1 \xrightarrow{E} \ptp_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{comp}\ \hat{e} : \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash []: [\ptp]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: [\ptp]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1 :: v_2: [\ptp]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Text] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vtxt{v}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Tag] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: [\tattr]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: [\tview]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vtag{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Attr] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tstr
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vattr{v_1}{v_2}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Click] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tone \arrow{E} \tone
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vclick{v}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\grey{TV-Set}] {
  \ell: \ptp \in \Pi[p]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \tset{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}

Expression judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid E$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp_1
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tset{\ptp}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \tone \mid \emut
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\ptp_1]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_2 \mid E
  \\
  \Gamma, x_\mf{hd}: \ptp_1, x_\mf{tl}: [\ptp_1] \semi \Pi
  \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}

Body judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\and
