\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
  \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}

Runtime value judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \rtp$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Var] {
  x: \rtp \in \Gamma
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash x: \rtp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash []: [\ttree]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Lit] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash \elit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u_1: \ttree
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u_2: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u_1 :: u_2: [\ttree]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Node] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\tattr]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash \enode{id}{v}{u}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Cell] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Pi[p]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash \mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

Runtime expression judgment: $\Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \rho: \rtp$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Ret] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \rtp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eret{u}: \rtp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \rho_1: \rtp_1
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \rtp_1 \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \rho_2: \rtp_2
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \elet{x}{\rho_1}{\rho_2}: \rtp_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Init] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tview
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \einit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Inits] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\tview]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \einits{v}: [\ttree]
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Scan] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tview
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \escan{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Scans] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\tview]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \escans{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Chk] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \echk{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Chks] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \echks{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Sr] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \esearch{id}{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Srs] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: [\ttree]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \esearches{id}{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell \cup \Pi[p]
  \and
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Pi[p]
  \and
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell \subseteq \Pi[p]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Hdl] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tone \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \ehdl{e}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Prog] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \eprog{e}: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}

Memory typing:

Define $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash \varsigma: \Ell$ 
if $\dom(\Ell)=\dom(\varsigma)$
and, for all $\ell \in \dom(\varsigma)$,
we have $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \varsigma[\ell]: \Ell[\ell]$.

Define $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$
if $\dom(\Pi)=\dom(m)$
and, for all $p \in \dom(m)$,
we have $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m[p].\mf{st}: \Pi[p]$.

\subsubsection{Semantics}

Expression judgment: $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=E-Let] {
  \estep{m}{e_1}{e_1'}{m'}
} {
  \estep{m}{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}}{\elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}}{m'}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=E-AppS] {
  m' = m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v\ \mf{and}\ m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt
} {
  \estep{m}{\vset{\ell}{p}\ v}{\eret{()}}{m'}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\estep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{e}&}{e[v/x]}{m}
&&\ts{E-Ret}\\
\estep{m}{(\lambda x. e)\ v&}{e[v/x]}{m}
&&\ts{E-AppL}\\
\estep{m}{\ecase{[]}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{h} :: x_\mf{t}}{e_2}&}{e_1}{m}
&&\ts{E-CaseN}\\
\estep{m}{\ecase{v_\mf{h} :: v_\mf{t}}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{h} :: x_\mf{t}}{e_2}&}
{e_2[v_\mf{h} / x_\mf{h}, v_\mf{t} / x_\mf{t}]}{m}
&&\ts{E-CaseC}
\end{align*}

Body judgment: $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  \estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}
} {
  \bstep{m}{e}{\phi}{p}{e'}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  \estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}
} {
  \bstep{m}{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}{\phi}{p}{\elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}}{m}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\bstep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}&}{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{m}
&&\ts{EB-Ret}\\
\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}&}{\mf{Init}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m'}
&&\ts{EB-StI}\\
(m' &= m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v)\\
\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}&}{\mf{Succ}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v'/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m}
&&\ts{EB-StS}\\
(v' &= m[p].\mf{st}[\ell])
\end{align*}

Runtime judgment: $m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow \rho' \semi m'$

Admin:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Let] {
  m \semi \rho_1 \hookrightarrow \rho_1' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \elet{x}{\rho_1}{\rho_2}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\rho_1'}{\rho_2} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \elet{x}{\eret{u}}{\rho}
&\hookrightarrow
\rho[u/x] \semi m
&&\ts{ER-Ret}\\
\end{align*}

Initialization:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ITag] {
  x\ \mf{fresh}
  \and
  \mf{id}\ \mf{fresh}
} {
  m \semi \einit{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\einits{v_2}}{\eret{\enode{id}{v_1}{x}}} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ISpec] {
  p\ \mf{fresh}
  \and
  m'=m[p \mapsto \{
    \mf{st}: \cdot, 
    \mf{rerender}: \mf{ff}
  \}]
} {
  m \semi \einit{\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}}
  \hookrightarrow
  \hat{e} \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \einit{\vtxt{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{\elit{v}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ILit}\\
m \semi \einits{[]}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{[]} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-INil}\\
m \semi \einits{v :: vs}
&\hookrightarrow
(\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\\&\quad\
  \elet{xs}{\einits{vs}}{&&\ts{ER-ICons}\\&\quad\
    \eret{x :: xs}
  }
}) \semi m
\end{align*}

Component Evaluation:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\phi}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}
} {
  m \semi \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}
  \hookrightarrow
  \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \eret{v}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}_0, x)} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-EDone}
\end{align*}

Event Handling:
\begin{align*}
m \semi \esearch{id}{\elit{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SLit}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
&\hookrightarrow
\esearch{id}{u} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SPath}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\enode{id'}{v}{u}}
&\hookrightarrow
\esearches{id}{u} \semi m\quad (\mf{id} \neq \mf{id}')
&&\ts{ER-SNodeF}\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\enode{id}{v}{u}}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\escans{v}}{\esearches{id}{u}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SNodeT}\\
m \semi \esearches{id}{[]}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SNil}\\
m \semi \esearches{id}{u :: us}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\esearch{id}{u}}{\esearch{id}{us}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-SCons}
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \escan{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScAttr}\\
m \semi \escan{\vclick{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\ehdl{v\ ()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScClick}\\
m \semi \escans{[]}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScNil}\\
m \semi \escans{u :: us}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{\_}{\escan{u}}{\escans{us}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ScCons}
\end{align*}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Hdl] {
  \estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}
} {
  m \semi \ehdl{e} \hookrightarrow \ehdl{e'} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \ehdl{\eret{()}} 
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{()} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-HDone}\\
\end{align*}

Checking:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkPF] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
} {
  m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
  \hookrightarrow
  \elet{x}{\echk{u}}{\eret{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, x)}} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=ER-ChkPT] {
  m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt
} {
  m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
  \hookrightarrow
  \hat{e} \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}
  \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \echk{\elit{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\eret{\elit{v}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ChkLit}\\
m \semi \echk{\enode{id}{v}{u}}
&\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\echk{u}&&\ts{ER-ChkNode}\\&\quad\ }
{\eret{\enode{id}{v}{x}}} \semi m\\
m \semi \echks{[]} &\hookrightarrow \eret{[]} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-ChkNil}\\
m \semi \echks{u :: us}
&\hookrightarrow
(\elet{x}{\echk{v}}{\\&\quad\
  \elet{xs}{\echks{vs}}{&&\ts{ER-ChkCons}\\&\quad\
    \eret{x :: xs}
  }
}) \semi m
\end{align*}

Programs:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Prog] {
  \cdot \semi e \mapsto e' \semi \cdot
} {
  \cdot \semi \eprog{e} \mapsto \eprog{e'} \semi \cdot
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \eprog{\eret{v}}
&\hookrightarrow
\einit{v} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-PDone}
\end{align*}

Top-Level:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Top] {
  m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow \rho' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \rho \hookrightarrow_0 \rho' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \eret{u} 
&\hookrightarrow_0
\elet{\_}{\esearch{id}{u}}{\echk{u}} \semi m
&&\ts{ER-Event}
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Translation}

Types:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\tone} &= \tone\\
\llrr{\tstr} &= \tstr\\
\llrr{A \xrightarrow{E} A} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \llrr{A}\\
\llrr{\mf{List}\ A} &= \mf{List}\ \llrr{A}\\
\llrr{\tview} &= \tview\\
\llrr{\tattr} &= \tattr\\
\llrr{\tset{A}} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone\\
\llrr{\ttree} &= \ttree
\end{align*}
Abbreviate, in the target language,
$A_\mf{res}=\tview \times \tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$
(the return type of initial-phase body translations.)

$\llrr{\Gamma}$ is defined pointwise; $\llrr{\Pi}$ is defined as:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\Pi} &= \bigcup_p\left( \{\I(p) \mapsto \tbool\}
\cup \bigcup_{\ell \in \pi.\mf{labs}} \{\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{\Ell[\ell]}\}
\right)\\
\end{align*}

Values:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{x} &= x\\
\llrr{()} &= ()\\
\llrr{s} &= s\\
\llrr{\lambda x.e} &= \lambda x.\llrr{e}\\
\llrr{\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}} &= \mf{comp}(
  \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}}
)\\
\llrr{[]} &= []\\
\llrr{v_1 :: v_2} &= \llrr{v_1} :: \llrr{v_2}\\
\llrr{\vtxt{v}} &= \vtxt{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}} &= \vtag{\llrr{v_1}}{\llrr{v_2}}\\
\llrr{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}} &= \vattr{\llrr{v_1}}{\llrr{v_2}}\\
\llrr{\vclick{v}} &= \vclick{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}
&= \lambda x. \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x}
\semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}}
\end{align*}

Expressions:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\eret{v}} &= \eret{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}} &= \elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}}\\
\llrr{v_1\ v_2} &= \llrr{v_1}\llrr{v_2}\\
\llrr{\ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}}
&= \ecase{\llrr{v}}{[]}{\llrr{e_1}}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{\llrr{e_2}}
\end{align*}

Bodies:

Intuitively, component bodies have two different interpretations:
under $\phi=\mf{Init}$, $\mf{useState}$ \emph{initializes}
a reference, and under $\phi=\mf{Succ}$, $\mf{useState}$
\emph{accesses} a reference.

To model this in a safe way, the translation of $\phi=\mf{Init}$
\emph{returns} the translation of $\phi=\mf{Succ}$,
such that the latter can \emph{close over} the locations
initialized by the former.

A lot of machinery is necessary to achieve this in a way
such that we can reason about intermediate stages of body evaluation.
For instance, due to the introduction of fresh variables,
we parameterize by a context
$$\delta ::= \emptyset \mid \delta, \ell \mapsto x 
\mid \delta, \mf{flag} \mapsto x$$
and define an auxiliary function as follows:
\[\delta(p, \ell) = \begin{cases}
  x & \text{if}\ \ell \mapsto x \in \delta\\
  \I(p, \ell) & \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\]
\[\delta(p) = \begin{cases}
  x & \text{if}\ \mf{flag} \mapsto x \in \delta\\
  \I(p) & \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\]
Intuitively, if a variable is in the range of $\delta$,
it was bound previously in the translation;
if not, then we have some raw location (given by $\I$)
instead.

\begin{align*}
\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top &= \elet{x_f}{\eref{\mf{false}}}
{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/p}_{\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  (x_\ell\ \mf{fresh})\quad&\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad
      \eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\delta(p)}{\mf{true}}}\\&\quad\ }{
       \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}
    }
  }
}\\
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x_r}{\llrr{e}}{\\
  &\quad\ \eret{(x_r,\delta(p),\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta)}
}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x}{\eget{\delta(p, \ell)}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad \eset{\delta(p, \ell)}{y} 
        \semi 
        \eset{\delta(p)}{\mf{true}}
      \\&\quad\
    }}
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}
\\
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta &= \llrr{e}
\end{align*}

Runtime values:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{x} &= x\\
\llrr{()} &= ()\\
\llrr{[]} &= []\\
\llrr{u :: u} &= \llrr{u} :: \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\elit{v}} &= \elit{\llrr{v}}\\
\llrr{\enode{id}{v}{u}} &= \enode{id}{\llrr{v}}{\llrr{u}}\\
\llrr{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)} 
&= \mf{cell}(
  \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\emptyset,
  \I(p),
  \llrr{u}
)
\end{align*}

Runtime expressions:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\eret{u}} &= \eret{\llrr{u}}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{\rho_1}{\rho}} &= 
\elet{x}{\llrr{\rho_1}}{\llrr{\rho_2}}\\
\llrr{\einit{v}} &= \mf{init}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\einits{v}} &= \mf{map}\ \mf{init}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\escan{v}} &= \mf{scan}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\escans{v}} &= \mf{iter}\ \mf{scan}\ \llrr{v}\\
\llrr{\echk{u}} &= \mf{check}\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\echks{u}} &= \mf{iter}\ \mf{check}\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\esearch{id}{u}} &= \mf{search}\ \mf{id}\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\esearches{id}{u}} &= \mf{iter}\ (\mf{search}\ \mf{id})\ \llrr{u}\\
\llrr{\hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}}
&= \elet{\mf{view, flag, spec}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\emptyset}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{
    \mf{cell(spec, flag, tree)}
  }
}\\
\llrr{\hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}}
&= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}}_p}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{
    \mf{cell}(
      \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\emptyset,
      \I(p),
      \mf{tree}
    )
  }
}\\
\llrr{\ehdl{e}} &= \llrr{e}\\
\llrr{\eprog{e}} &= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{e}}{\einit{\mf{view}}}
\end{align*}
(Constants to be defined: 
$\mf{init}, \mf{scan}, \mf{search}, \mf{check}, \mf{map}, \mf{iter}$)

Memory:
$$
\llrr{m} = \bigcup_{p \in m} \left(
  \{\I(p) \mapsto \llrr{\pi}.\mf{rerender}\}
  \cup \bigcup_{\ell \in m[p].\mf{st}} \{
    \I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]}
  \}
\right)
$$

\section{Proofs, Again}

\subsection{Source Type Soundness}

\begin{theorem}[Progress, expressions.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid E$ and
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vDash m$,
\end{enumerate}
then either:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $e = \eret{v}$ or
\item $m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'$ for some $e', m'$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction over the derivation of the expression typing
premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Ret}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We trivially satisfy conclusion \emph{(i)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Let}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E
  }
  \and
  x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)}, 
we have two subcases:
either (a) $e_1=\eret{v}$
or (b) $m \semi e_1 \mapsto e_1' \semi m'$.
\item In subcase (a), rule \ts{E-Ret}
says $m \semi \elet{x}{\eret{v}}{e_2} \mapsto e_2[v/x] \semi m$.
\item In subcase (b), we derive a step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=E-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(b)}] { } {\estep{m}{e_1}{e_1'}{m'}}
} {
  \estep{m}{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}}{\elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}}{m'}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either case, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppL}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
  }
  \and
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp_1
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (canonical forms) on (1),
we have that $v_1 = \lambda x.e$.
\item We apply rule \ts{E-AppL} to get that
$m \semi (\lambda x.e)\ v \mapsto e[v/x] \semi m$,
satisfying conclusion \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppS}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tset{\ptp}
  }
  \and
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \tone \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (canonical forms) on (1),
we have that $v_1 = \vset{\ell}{p}$.
\item We apply rule \ts{E-AppL} to get
$m \semi \vset{\ell}{p}\ v \mapsto 
\eret{()} \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v$,
satisfying conclusion \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Case}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: [\ptp_1]}
  \and
  \cdots
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash
  \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (canonical forms) on (1), we have two subcases:
either (a) $v=[]$ or (b) $v=v_\mf{hd} :: v_\mf{tl}$.
\item In subcase (a), we apply rule \ts{E-CaseN} to get
$$\estep{m}{\ecase{[]}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}}{e_1}{m}$$
\item In subcase (b), we apply rule \ts{E-CaseC} to get
$$\estep{m}{\ecase{v_\mf{h} :: v_\mf{t}}{[]}{e_1}
{x_\mf{h} :: x_\mf{t}}{e_2}}
{e_2[v_\mf{h} / x_\mf{h}, v_\mf{t} / x_\mf{t}]}{m}$$
\item Thus, in either subcase, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Sub}: We have \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item The result follows immediately from the inductive hypothesis
applied to (1) and premise \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Preservation, expressions.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid E$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$, and
\item $m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'$,
\end{enumerate}
then:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \ptp \mid E$ and
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m'$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the step premise \emph{(iii)}.

Note that conclusion \emph{(ii)} 
is immediate by premise \emph{(ii)} when $m'=m$.

\tb{Case} \ts{E-Let}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=E-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e_1}{e_1'}{m'}}
} {
  \estep{m}{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}}{\elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}}{m'}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {\Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1), (2), and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get that $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m'$,
satisfying conclusion \emph{(ii)},
and (4) that $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1': \ptp \mid E$.
\item We derive conclusion \emph{(i)} as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1': \ptp_1 \mid E
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-Ret}: We have that
$\estep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{e}}{e[v/x]}{m}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item (1) lets us derive (3) $\tval{\Gamma}{\Pi}{v}{\ptp_1}$
by subcasing on the value of $E$:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule* {(3)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v : \ptp_1
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v} : \ptp_1 \mid \epsilon
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule* {(3)} {
      \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v : \ptp_1
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v} : \ptp_1 \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp_1 \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
\item By \hl{REF} (substitution)
on (2) and (3), we get
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e[v/x]: \ptp_2 \mid E$ and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-AppL}: We have that
$\estep{m}{(\lambda x.e)\ v}{e[v/x]}{m}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {
      \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp_2 \mid E
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.e: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp_1
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash (\lambda x.e)\ v: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (substitution)
on (1) and (2), we get
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e[v/x]: \ptp_2 \mid E$ and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-AppS}: We have 
$\estep{m}{\vset{\ell}{p}\ v}{\eret{()}}{m'}$,
where
$$m' = m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v\ 
\mf{and}\ m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt$$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {
      \ell: \ptp \in \Pi[p]
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \tset{\ptp}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}\ v: \tone \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item To show conclusion \emph{(ii)} given premise \emph{(ii)},
it suffices to show that 
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash m'[p].\mf{st}[\ell]: \Pi[p][\ell]$.
\item By (1) and definition of $m'$,
it suffices to show that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp$.
This is given by (2).
\item Conclusion \emph{(i)} is derived as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
      \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash (): \tone
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{()}: \tone \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{()}: \tone \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-CaseN}: We have
$\estep{m}{\ecase{[]}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{h} :: x_\mf{t}}{e_2}}{e_1}{m}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp \mid E
  }
  \and
  \cdots
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \ecase{[]}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}: \ptp \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Conclusion \emph{(i)} is given directly by (1).
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-CaseC}: We have
$$\estep{m}{\ecase{v_\mf{hd} 
:: v_\mf{tl}}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}}
{e_2[v_\mf{hd} / x_\mf{hd}, v_\mf{tl} / x_\mf{tl}]}{m}$$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  \inferrule* {\vdots} {(1)}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x_\mf{hd}: \ptp_1, x_\mf{tl} : \mf{List}\ \ptp_1
    \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_2 \mid E
  }
  \and
  \cdots
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \ecase{v_\mf{h} :: v_\mf{t}}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \inferrule* {(3)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_\mf{hd}: \ptp_1
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(4)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_\mf{tl}: \mf{List}\ \ptp_1
  }
} {
  (1) \quad
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_\mf{hd} :: v_\mf{tl}: \mf{List}\ \ptp_1
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We derive conclusion \emph{(i)} via two applications of
\hl{REF} (substitution) to (2) using (3) and (4).
\end{itemize}
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Progress, bodies, initial.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$ and
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vDash m$,
\end{enumerate}
then either:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\hat{e}=\eret{v}$ or
\item $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the body typing premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Lift}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon}
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression progress) 
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get that either $e=\eret{v}$
(and we are done by conclusion \emph{(i)}),
or (2) that $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}$.
\item By \hl{REF} (purity) on (2) and premise \emph{(i)},
we get that $m'=m$, i.e. (3) $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}$.
\item In the latter case, we derive conclusion \emph{(ii)}
as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{e}{\mf{Init}}{p}{e'}{m}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Let}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression progress) 
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get that either (a) $e=\eret{v}$
or (b) that $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{EB-Ret} gives that
$\bstep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{m}$.
\item In subcase (b), we derive a step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(\tn{b})] { } {
    \estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}
  }
} {
  \bstep{m}{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Init}}{p}
  {\elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}}{m}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either subcase, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-State}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
  \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We can immediately apply \ts{EB-StI} 
to satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}:
\end{itemize}
$$
\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Init}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v}
$$
\
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Preservation, bodies, initial.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$, and
\item $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$,
\end{enumerate}
then there exists some $\Pi' \rpo \Pi$ such that:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash \hat{e}': \Ell'$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vDash m'$, and
\item $\Ell' \cup \Pi'[p] = \Ell \cup \Pi[p]$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the step judgment \emph{(iii)}.

Unless otherwise mentioned, we choose $\Pi'=\Pi$,
such that conclusion \emph{(ii)} is given by premise \emph{(ii)}
if $m'=m$,
and conclusion \emph{(iii)} holds if $\Ell'=\Ell$.

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Lift}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{e}{\mf{Init}}{p}{e'}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression preservation)
on (1), (2), and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) that 
$\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash e': \tview \mid \epsilon$.
\item We derive conclusion \emph{(i)} as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Let}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}{\phi}{p}{\elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression preservation) on (1), (2),
and premise \emph{(ii)}, we get (4) that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \ptp \mid \epsilon$.
\item We derive conclusion \emph{(i)} as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Ret}: We have
$\bstep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}}
{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{m}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {
      \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Conclusion \emph{(i)} follows from \hl{REF} (substitution)
applies to (1) and (2).
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-StI}: We have
$$\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Init}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m'}$$
where $m' = m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v$,
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
    \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Choose $\Pi'=\Pi[p \mapsto \Pi[p], \ell: A]$;
clearly $\Pi \po \Pi'$.
\item We show conclusion \emph{(i)} as follows:
\begin{itemize}
  \item By \hl{REF} ($\Pi$-weakening) on (1),
  we get (3) that $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash v: \ptp$.
  \item Similarly, by \hl{REF} ($\Pi$-weakening) on (2),
  we obtain (4) that $\Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} 
  \semi \Pi' \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$.
  \item Since $\ell: A \in \Pi'[p]$ by definition,
  we derive the setter type as follows:
  \begin{mathpar}
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
    \ell: A \in \Pi'[p]
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \tset{A}
    \quad(5)
  }
  \end{mathpar}
  \item We apply \hl{REF} (substitution) twice to (4),
  using (3) and (5), to get that
  $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash 
  \hat{e}[v/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]: \Ell$,
  i.e. conclusion \emph{(i)} holds.
\end{itemize}
\item We show conclusion \emph{(ii)} as follows:
\begin{itemize}
  \item We want (6) that $\dom(\Pi') = \dom(m')$. 
  By definition of each, it suffices to show that 
  $\dom(\Pi) \cup \{p\} = \dom(m) \cup \{p\}$.
  Premise \emph{(ii)} says that $\dom(\Pi) = \dom(m)$, 
  so this holds.
  \item We also want (7) that $\dom(\Pi'[p]) = \dom(m'[p].\mf{st})$.
  Again by definition of each, it suffices to show that 
  $\dom(\Pi[p]) \cup \{\ell\} = \dom(m[p].\mf{st}) \cup \{\ell\}$.
  Premise \emph{(ii)} says that $\dom(\Pi[p]) = \dom(m[p].\mf{st})$, 
  so this holds.
  \item Finally, we want (8) that
  $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash m'[p].\mf{st}[\ell]: \Pi[p][\ell]$.
  By definition, it suffices that
  $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash v: A$,
  which is given by (3).
  \item By premise \emph{(ii)} and by definition,
  (6)-(8) are sufficient to show that
  $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vDash m'$,
  i.e. conclusion \emph{(ii)} holds.
\end{itemize}
\item Finally, we show conclusion \emph{(iii)} as follows:
\begin{itemize}
  \item We want to show that 
  $\Ell \cup \Pi'[p] = \Ell, \ell: A \cup \Pi[p]$.
  \item By definition, it suffices to show that 
  $\Ell \cup \Pi[p], \ell: A = \Ell, \ell: A \cup \Pi[p]$.
  This follows by definition of label-context 
  composition and equality, and we are done.
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-StS}: Impossible, since this rule
requires that $\phi=\mf{Succ}$.
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Progress, bodies, successor.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vDash m$, and
\item $\Ell \subseteq \Pi[p]$,
\end{enumerate}
then either:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\hat{e}=\eret{v}$ or
\item $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m}$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the body typing premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Lift}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon}
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression progress) 
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get that either $e=\eret{v}$
(and we are done by conclusion \emph{(i)}),
or (2) that $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m'}$.
\item By \hl{REF} (purity) on (2) and premise \emph{(i)},
we get that $m'=m$, i.e. (3) $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}$.
\item In the latter case, we derive conclusion \emph{(ii)}
as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{e}{\mf{Succ}}{p}{e'}{m}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Let}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression progress) 
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get that either (a) $e=\eret{v}$
or (b) that $\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{EB-Ret} gives that
$\bstep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{m}$.
\item In subcase (b), we derive a step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(\tn{b})] { } {
    \estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}
  }
} {
  \bstep{m}{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}
  {\elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}}{m}
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either subcase, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-State}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
  \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item We want to apply rule \ts{EB-StS}, which says that:
$$\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v'/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m}$$
where $v' = m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]$. To do so, we must show
that $v'$ is well-defined.
\item Premise \emph{(iii)}, alongside the type of the body,
tells us that $\ell: A \in \Pi[p]$.
\item Then by premise \emph{(ii)}, we get that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]: A$,
i.e. $m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]$ is well-defined
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Preservation, bodies, successor.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$
(where $\Ell \subseteq \Pi[p]$),
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$, and
\item $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m}$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}': \Ell'$ 
for some $\Ell' \subseteq \Ell$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the step judgment \emph{(iii)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Lift}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{e}{\mf{Init}}{p}{e'}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression preservation)
on (1), (2), and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash e': \tview \mid \epsilon$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Let}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}{\phi}{p}{\elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (expression preservation) on (1), (2),
and premise \emph{(ii)}, we get (4) that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \ptp \mid \epsilon$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e': \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Ret}: We have
$\bstep{m}{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}}
{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}[v/x]}{m}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {
      \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
    }
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item The conclusion follows from \hl{REF} (substitution)
applies to (1) and (2).
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-StI}: Impossible, since this rule
requires that $\phi=\mf{Init}$.

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-StS}: We have
$$\bstep{m}{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}{\mf{Init}}{p}
{\hat{e}[v'/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]}{m}$$
where $v' = m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]$,
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
    \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Since $v'$ is well-defined, we have by premise \emph{(ii)}
that (2) $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v': \Pi[p][\ell]$.
\item Since $\Ell, \ell: A \subseteq \Pi[p]$, 
we have (3) that $\Pi[p][\ell]=A$.
\item (2) and (3) give us (4)
that $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v': A$.
\item We derive the setter type as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {\ell: A \in \Pi[p]}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \tset{A}
  \quad (5)
}
\end{mathpar}
\item We apply \hl{REF} (substitution) twice to (1),
using (4) and (5), to get that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}[v'/x,\vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]: \Ell$,
satisfying the conclusion.
\end{itemize}

\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Progress, runtime.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \rho: \ptp$ and
\item $\cdot \semi \Pi \vDash m$,
\end{enumerate}
then either:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\rho=\eret{u}$ or
\item $m \semi \rho \mapsto \rho' \semi m'$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the expression typing premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalI}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell \cup \Pi[p]
  \and
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (initial-phase body progress) on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we have either (a) that $\hat{e}=\eret{v}$ 
or (b) that $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{ER-EDone} gives us that
$m \semi \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \eret{v}
\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}_0, x)} \semi m$.
\item In subcase (b), we derive a step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(b)}] { } {
    \bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Init}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}
  }
} {
  m \semi \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}
  \hookrightarrow
  \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either subcase, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalS}: We have:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Pi[p]
  \and
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Ell \subseteq \Pi[p]}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By \hl{REF} (successor-phase body progress) on (1), (2),
and premise \emph{(ii)},
we have either (a) that $\hat{e}=\eret{v}$ 
or (b) that $\bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}$.
\item In subcase (a), \ts{ER-EDone} gives us that
$m \semi \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \eret{v}
\hookrightarrow
\elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}_0, x)} \semi m$.
\item In subcase (b), we derive a step as follows:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=ER-Eval] {
  \inferrule*[right=\tn{(b)}] { } {
    \bstep{m}{\hat{e}}{\mf{Succ}}{p}{\hat{e}'}{m'}
  }
} {
  m \semi \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}
  \hookrightarrow
  \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\item Thus, in either subcase, we satisfy conclusion \emph{(ii)}
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{All other cases}: \hl{TODO}; comparatively straightforward,
but should fill in.

\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Preservation, runtime.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \rho: \ptp$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$, and
\item $m \semi \rho \mapsto \rho' \semi m'$,
\end{enumerate}
then there exists some $\Pi' \rpo \Pi$ such that:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vdash \rho': \ptp$ and
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \vDash m'$.
\end{enumerate}


\end{theorem}

\subsection{Runtime Relations}

Define $\mathbb{B}: \mathbb{N} \rightharpoonup \mf{Loc}$ to be a
partial function from paths $p$ in the source language to locations $r$
in the target language.

Similarly define $\mathbb{L}: (\mathbb{N} \times \mathbb{N}) 
\rightharpoonup \mf{Loc}$ as a
partial function from path/label pairs $(p, \ell)$ in the source language 
to locations $r$ in the target language.

Let $\I=\pair{\mathbb{B}}{\mathbb{L}}$.
We overload $\I$ such that
$\I(p)=\mathbb{B}(p)$ and 
$\I(p, \ell)=\mathbb{L}(p, \ell)$.

Now we need some notion of a \emph{sufficient} interpretation $\I$.
For instance, the empty interpretation is sufficient
to translate $\llrr{\vtxt{s}}=\vtxt{s}$, but not to
translate 
$\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}=\lambda x.(\eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x} 
\semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}})$,
since e.g. $\I(p, \ell)=\bot$.

Define $\Pi \vDash \I$ to hold when:
\begin{itemize}
\item $\I(p) \neq \bot \Leftrightarrow p \in \dom(\Pi)$, and
\item $\I(p, \ell) \neq \bot \Leftrightarrow \ell \in \Pi[p]$.
\end{itemize}

We want to show the \emph{well-formedness}
of each translation function parameterized by $\I$.
(By well-formed, we mean that any instance of an
interpretation function $\I(p)$ or $\I(p, \ell)$ resolves to a concrete
location $r$ in the target language.)

\begin{lemma}[Well-definedness, pointer context.]
If $\Pi \vDash \I$, then $\llrr{\Pi}_\I$ is well-defined.

\begin{Proof}
Recall our definition of $\llrr{\Pi}_\I$:
\[
\llrr{\Pi}_\I = \bigcup_p\left( \{\I(p) \mapsto \tbool\}
\cup \bigcup_{\ell \in \Pi[p]} \{\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{\Pi[p][\ell]}\}
\right)\\
\]
\begin{itemize}
\item First consider arbitrary $p \in \dom(\Pi)$.
By the definition of the premise, we have that $\I(p) \neq \bot$,
i.e. there exists some target-language location $r$ such that
$\I(p)=r$.
\item Next, for that $p$, consider arbitrary $\ell \in \Pi[p]$.
Again by definition of the premise, we have that
$\I(p, \ell) \neq \bot$, 
i.e. there exists some target-language location $r_\ell$ such that
$\I(p, \ell)=r_\ell$.
\item Now by definition, every invocation of $\I$ within $\llrr{\Pi}_\I$
resolves to a concrete location, and we are done.
\end{itemize}
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\subsection{Type Preservation}

We prove \emph{type preservation lemmas}
for each term-level translation.
Each lemma has a corresponding 
\emph{well-definedness corollary},
which states that the translation result is well defined;
this always follows from the fact that undefined terms
cannot be typed.

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, values.]
\label{thm:tprezval}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v}_\I: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of premise \emph{(i)}.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with \hl{REFS}, type preservation for expressions and bodies.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Var}: We have $\llrr{x}_\I=x$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {x: \ptp \in \Gamma}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash x: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1) and definition of $\llrr{\Gamma}$, we get (2) that
$x: \llrr{\ptp} \in \llrr{\Gamma}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {x: \llrr{\ptp} \in \llrr{\Gamma}}
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v x: \llrr{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Unit}: $\llrr{()}_\I=()$ and $\llrr{\tone}=\tone$,
so the conclusion is easy:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v (): \llrr{\tone}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Str}: $\llrr{s}_\I=s$ and $\llrr{\tstr}=\tstr$,
so the conclusion is easy:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \llrr{s}_\I: \llrr{\tstr}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Lam}: We have
\begin{itemize}
\item $\llrr{\lambda x.e}_\I=\lambda x.\llrr{e}_\I$,
\item $\llrr{\ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2} 
= \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}$,
\end{itemize}
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp_2 \mid E
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.e: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\hl{REF}) on (1),
we get $\llrr{\Gamma, x: \ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
\vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_2}$.
\item By definition, this is equivalent to (2) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_2}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_2}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \lambda x.\llrr{e}_\I: 
  \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Comp}: We have 
\begin{itemize}
\item $\llrr{\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}}=\mf{comp}\ \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I$,
\item $\llrr{\tview} = \tview$,
\end{itemize}
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{comp}\ \hat{e} : \tview
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis on (1) (Theorem \hl{REF}),
we get (2) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I: A_\mf{res}$.
\item We can derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
      \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
      \vdash_e
      \llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I: A_\mf{res}
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I: \tone \rightarrow A_\mf{res}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_v
  \mf{comp}\ \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Nil}: $\llrr{[]}_\I=[]$
and $\llrr{\mf{List}\ \ptp}=\mf{List}\ \llrr{\ptp}$,
so the conclusion is easy:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash []: \mf{List}\ \ptp
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v []: \mf{List}\ \llrr{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Cons}: We have 
$\llrr{v :: vs}_\I = \llrr{v}_\I :: \llrr{vs}_\I$ 
and $\llrr{\mf{List}\ \ptp}=\mf{List}\ \llrr{\ptp}$, and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \mf{List}\ \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1 :: v_2: \mf{List}\ \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on each of (1) and (2),
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp}$
and (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_2}_\I: \mf{List}\ \llrr{\ptp}$ respectively.
\item We then derive the conclusion:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v v_2: \mf{List}\ \llrr{\ptp}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
  \vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I :: \llrr{v_2}_\I: \mf{List}\ \llrr{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Cases} \ts{TV-Text}, \ts{TV-Tag},
\ts{TV-Attr}, \ts{TV-Click}: \hl{elided} for the moment,
but should be easy.

\tb{Case} \ts{TV-Set}: We have
\begin{itemize}
\item $\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}_\I = \lambda x. \eset{\mathbb{I}(p, \ell)}{x}
\semi \eset{\mathbb{I}(p)}{\mf{true}}$,
\item $\llrr{\tset{A}} = \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone$,
\end{itemize}
and: \begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\ell: A \in \Pi[p]}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \tset{\ptp}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1) and premise \emph{(ii)}, we get that
$\I(p)$ and $\I(p, \ell)$ are each well-defined.
\item Furthermore, by definition of $\llrr{\Pi}$,
we get (3) that $\I(p, \ell) : \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}$
and (4) that $\I(p) : \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \I(p, \ell): \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}
  }
} {
  (5)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \I(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{A}}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \I(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}
  }
} {
  (6)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
  \I(p): \tref{\tbool}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  (5)
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v x: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  (7)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
  \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  (6)
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=TV-True] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} 
    \vdash_v \mf{true}: \tbool
  }
} {
  (8)\quad \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e 
  \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
    (7)
    \and
    (8)
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_e
    \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x}
    \semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}} : \tone
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v 
  \lambda x. \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x}
  \semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}} : \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
}
\end{mathpar}\
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, values.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{v}_\I$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, expressions.]
\label{thm:tprezexp}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A \mid E$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of premise \emph{(i)}.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with \hl{REFS}, type preservation for values and bodies.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Ret}: We have
$\llrr{\eret{v}}_\I = \eret{\llrr{v}_\I}$ and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eret{v}: \ptp \mid \epsilon
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:tprezval},
value type preservation) on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (2) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v}_\I: \llrr{A}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v 
    \llrr{v}_\I: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \eret{\llrr{v}_\I}: \llrr{A}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Let}: We have
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}}_\I=\elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}_\I}{\llrr{e_2}_\I}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{e_1}_\I: \llrr{A_1}$.
\item Again by inductive hypothesis on (2) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma, x: \ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{e_2}_\I: \llrr{A_2}$.
\item By definition of $\llrr{\Gamma}$, (4) is equal to (5) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{\ptp_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{e_2}_\I: \llrr{A_2}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_e
    \llrr{e_1}_\I: \llrr{A_1}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(5)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A_1} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_e
    \llrr{e_2}_\I: \llrr{A_2}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}_\I}{\llrr{e_2}_\I}: \llrr{A_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppL}: We have
$\llrr{v_1\ v_2}_\I = \llrr{v_1}_\I \llrr{v_2}_\I$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppL] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp_1
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:tprezval},
value type preservation) on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_1 \arrow{E} \ptp_2}$.
\item By definition, (3) is equal to (4) that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}$.
\item Again by mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:tprezval})
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (5) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_2}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_1}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_1} \rightarrow \llrr{\ptp_2}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(5)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \llrr{v_2}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_1}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_v 
  \llrr{v_1}_\I \llrr{v_2}_\I: \llrr{\ptp_2}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-AppS}: We have
$\llrr{v_1\ v_2}_\I = \llrr{v_1}_\I \llrr{v_2}_\I$,
$\llrr{\tone}=\tone$,
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: \tset{\ptp}}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: \ptp}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: \tone \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:tprezval},
value type preservation) on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\tset{\ptp}}$.
\item By definition, (3) is equal to (4) that
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone$.
\item Again by mutual inductive hypothesis (\thref{thm:tprezval})
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (5) that $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{v_2}_\I: \llrr{\ptp}$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \llrr{v_1}_\I: \llrr{\ptp} \rightarrow \tone
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(5)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \llrr{v_2}_\I: \llrr{\ptp}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_v 
  \llrr{v_1}_\I \llrr{v_2}_\I: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TE-Case}: \hl{Elided}, but should fill in.
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, expressions.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A \mid E$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{e}_\I$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

To reason about $\delta$, we introduce the judgment
$\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
which holds when:
\begin{itemize}
\item for all $\ell: A \in \Ell$, either:
\begin{itemize}
  \item $\ell \mapsto x_\ell \in \delta$ 
  and $x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \in \Delta$, or
  \item $\ell \notin \dom(\delta)$ and $\ell: A \in \Pi[p]$,
\end{itemize}
\item and either:
\begin{itemize}
  \item $\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f \in \delta$ 
  and $x_f: \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta$, or
  \item $\mf{flag} \notin \dom(\delta)$ and $p \in \dom(\Pi)$.
\end{itemize}
\end{itemize}

We state some auxiliary lemmas about 
the typing of the $\delta$-function
before proceeding with type preservation:
\begin{lemma}[Sub-recording under delta.]
\label{thm:deltasub}

If $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$
and $\Ell' \po \Ell$,
then $\Ell' \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$

\begin{Proof}
Obvious by definition.
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[Flag-typing under delta.]
\label{thm:deltaflag}
If
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v 
\delta_\I(p): \tref{\tbool}$.

\begin{Proof}
By case-analysis on premise \emph{(i)}, either:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(i-\alph*)]
\item $\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f \in \delta$ 
and $x_f: \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta$, or
\item $\mf{flag} \notin \dom(\delta)$ and $p \in \dom(\Pi)$.
\end{enumerate}

\tb{Case} (i-a):
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, $\delta_\I(p)=x_f$.
\item From the premise, we also have 
(1) that $x_f: \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \inferrule*[right=(1)] { } {x_f: \tref{\tbool} \in \Delta}
} {
  \Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v x_f: \tref{\tbool}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} (i-b):
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, $\delta_\I(p)=\I(p)$.
\item By premise \emph{(ii)}, since $p \in \dom(\Pi)$,
we have that $\I(p)$ is well-defined.
\item Then by definition of $\llrr{\Pi}_\I$,
we get (2) that $\I(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}_\I$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {\I(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}_\I}
} {
  \Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \I(p): \tref{\tbool}
}
\end{mathpar}
Thus, in any case, we conclude that
$\Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \delta_\I(p): \tref{\tbool}$
and we are done.
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[Label-typing under delta.]
\label{thm:deltalab}
If
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$, and
\item $\ell: A \in \Ell$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v 
\delta_\I(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{A}}$.

\begin{Proof}
By case-analysis on premise \emph{(i)}, either:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(i-\alph*)]
\item $\ell \mapsto x_\ell \in \delta$ 
and $x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \in \Delta$, or
\item $\ell \notin \dom(\delta)$ and $\ell: A \in \Pi[p]$.
\end{enumerate}

\tb{Case} (i-a):
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, $\delta_\I(p, \ell)=x_\ell$.
\item From the premise, we also have 
(1) that $x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \in \Delta$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  \inferrule*[right=(1)] { } {
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \in \Delta
  }
} {
  \Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} (i-b):
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, $\delta_\I(p, \ell)=\I(p, \ell)$.
\item By premise \emph{(ii)}, since $\ell: A \in \Pi[p]$,
we have that $\I(p, \ell)$ is well-defined.
\item Then by definition of $\llrr{\Pi}_\I$,
we get (2) that $\I(p, \ell): \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}_\I$.
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \inferrule*[right=(2)] { } {
    \I(p, \ell): \llrr{A} \in \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  }
} {
  \Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \I(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{A}}
}
\end{mathpar}
Thus, in any case, we conclude that
$\Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \delta_\I(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{A}}$
and we are done.
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, bodies, successor phase.]
\label{thm:tprezbods}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$, and
\item $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \tview$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of premise \emph{(i)}.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with \hl{REFS}, type preservation for values and expressions.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Lift}: We have
$\llrr{e}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta} = \llrr{e}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis 
(\hl{REF}, expression type preservation)
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (2) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \tview$
(noting that $\llrr{\tview}=\tview$).
\item We weaken (2) by $\Delta$ (\hl{REF}) to get that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \tview$,
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Let}: We have
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}
= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis 
(\hl{REF}, expression type preservation)
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{A}$.
\item We weaken (4) by $\Delta$ (\hl{REF}) to get (4) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \tview$.
\item By inductive hypothesis 
on (2) and premises \emph{(ii)} and \emph{(iii)},
we get (5) that
$\llrr{\Gamma, x: A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \llrr{\tview}$.
\item By definition, (5) is equal to (6)
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \tview$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{A}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(6)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \tview
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
  \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}}: \tview
  \and (\ts{\small{TE-Let}})
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-State}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x}{\eget{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad \eset{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}{y} 
        \semi 
        \eset{\delta_\I(p)}{\mf{true}}
      \\&\quad\
    }}
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\delta}
\end{align*}
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
  \and
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
    \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item Note that $\Ell \po \Ell, \ell: \ptp$,
so by \thref{thm:deltasub} (sub-recording under $\delta$) 
on premise \emph{(iii)},
we get (2) that $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$.
\item By inductive hypothesis 
on (1), (2), and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (3) that
$\llrr{\Gamma, x: A, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp}}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
\vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \llrr{\tview}$.
\item By definition, (3) is equal to (4)
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, x_\mf{set}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone, \Delta 
\semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \tview$.
\item By \thref{thm:deltalab} (label-typing under $\delta$) on
premises \emph{(ii)} and \emph{(iii)},
we get (5) $\Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
\vdash_v \delta_\I(p, \ell): \tref{\llrr{A}}$.
\item By \thref{thm:deltaflag} (flag-typing under $\delta$) on
premises \emph{(ii)} and \emph{(iii)},
we get (6) $\Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
\vdash_v \delta_\I(p): \tref{\tbool}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion,
weakening appropriately (by \hl{REF}) along the way:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Get] {
  \inferrule*[right=(5)] { } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v \delta_\I(p, \ell) : \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
} {
  \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e \eget{\delta_\I(p, \ell)} : \llrr{A}
  \quad (7)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  \inferrule*[right=(5)] { } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v \delta_\I(p, \ell) : \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    y: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v y: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \Delta, y: \llrr{A} \vdash_e \eset{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}{y}: \tone
  \quad (8)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  \inferrule*[right=(6)] { } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v \delta_\I(p) : \tref{\tbool}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=TV-True] { } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \mf{true}: \tbool
  }
} {
  \Delta, \vdash_e \eset{\delta_\I(p)}{\mf{true}}: \tone
  \quad (9)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
      \inferrule* { } {(8)}
      \and
      \inferrule* { } {(9)}
    } {
      \Delta, y: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
      \vdash_v
      \eset{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}{y} 
      \semi \eset{\delta_\I(p)}{\mf{true}}: \tone
    }
  } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \lambda y.\cdots: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  }
} {
  \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \eret{\lambda y.\cdots}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  \quad (10)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* { } {(7)}
  \
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
    \inferrule* { } {(10)}
    \and
    \inferrule* { } {(4)}
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_e
    \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\cdots}{\cdots}: \tview
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x}{\eget{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}}{\cdots}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}

\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, bodies, successor phase.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$, and
\item $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, bodies, initial phase.]
\label{thm:tprezbodi}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell_0$
(where $\Ell \subseteq \Ell_0$),
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$, and
\item $(\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell) \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}: A_\mf{res}$.

\begin{Proof}
Recall that $A_\mf{res}=\tview \times 
\tref{\tbool} \times (\tone \rightarrow \tview)$.

We proceed by induction on the derivation of premise \emph{(i)}.
(This induction is \emph{mutual} 
with \hl{REFS}, type preservation for values and expressions.)

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Lift}: We have
$$
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}
= \elet{x_r}{\llrr{e}}{
  \eret{(x_r,\delta_\I(p),\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta})}
}
$$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis 
(\hl{REF}, expression type preservation)
on (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (2) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \tview$.
\item Since $\Ell=\emptyset$, premise \emph{(iv)}
reduces to (3) that $\Ell_0 \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$.
\item Then, mutual inductive hypothesis
(\hl{REF}, successor-phase body type preservation)
on (3) and premises \emph{(ii)} and \emph{(iii)},
we get (4)
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \tview$.
\item Also, by \thref{thm:deltaflag} (flag-typing under $\delta$)
applied to (3) and premise \emph{(iii)},
we get (5) that 
$\Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \delta_\I(p): \tref{\tbool}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion,
weakening appropriately (by \hl{REF}) along the way:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Pair] {
  \inferrule* { } {(5)}
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v
      \llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: \tview
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v
    \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}: 
    \tone \rightarrow \tview
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v
  (\delta(p), \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}): 
  \tref{\tbool} \times \tone \rightarrow \tview
  \quad (6)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Pair] {
    \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
      x_r: \tview \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v x_r: \tview
    }
    \and
    \inferrule* { } {(6)}
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_r: \tview \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
    \vdash_v
    (x_r, \delta(p), 
    \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta})
    : A_\mf{res}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta, x_r: \tview \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
  \vdash_e
  \eret{(x_r, \delta(p), 
  \lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta})}
  : A_\mf{res}
  \quad (7)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* { } {(3)}
  \and
  \inferrule* { } {(7)}
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I 
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x_r}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\eret{\cdots}}
  : A_\mf{res}
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-Let}: We have
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}
= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis 
(\hl{REF}, expression type preservation)
on (1) and premise \emph{(iii)},
we get (3) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{A}$.
\item We weaken (4) by $\Delta$ (\hl{REF}) to get (4) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: A_\mf{res}$.
\item By inductive hypothesis 
on (2) and premises \emph{(ii)}-\emph{(iv)},
we get (5) that
$\llrr{\Gamma, x: A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}: A_\mf{res}$.
\item By definition, (5) is equal to (6)
$\llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}: A_\mf{res}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e \llrr{e}_\I: \llrr{A}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=(6)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, x: \llrr{A}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}: A_\mf{res}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
  \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}}: 
  A_\mf{res}
  \and (\ts{\small{TE-Let}})
}
\end{mathpar}

\tb{Case} \ts{TB-State}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\delta
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}}}{\\
  (x_\ell\ \mf{fresh})\quad&\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad
      \eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\delta(p)}{\mf{true}}}\\&\quad\ }{
       \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}
    }
  }
}
\end{align*}
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp}
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp, x_\mf{set}: \tset{\ptp} \semi \Pi
    \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: \ptp
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By mutual inductive hypothesis 
(\hl{REF}, value type preservation)
on (1) and premise \emph{(iii)},
we get (3) that 
$\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \llrr{v}_\I: \llrr{A}$.
\item We weaken (4) by $\Delta$ (\hl{REF}) to get (5) that
$\llrr{\Gamma}, \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \llrr{v}_\I: \llrr{A}$.
\item Now we want to apply the inductive hypothesis to (2).
\item From premise \emph{(iv)}, that 
$(\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell, \ell: A) \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
we want some $\Delta'$ with (6)
$(\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell) \semi \Pi \vDash \delta, \ell \mapsto x_\ell: \Delta'$:
  \begin{itemize}
  \item Since $\Ell, \ell: A \po \Ell_0$, 
  we have that $\ell: A \in (\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell)$.
  \item Choose $\Delta'=\Delta,x_\ell: \llrr{A}$,
  such that (6) holds for $\ell$ by definition.
  \item Furthermore, by definition and by premise \emph{(iv)}, 
  (6) holds for all $\ell': A' \in (\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell,\ell: A)$.
  \end{itemize}
\item We apply the inductive hypothesis to (2), (6), 
and premises \emph{(ii)} and \emph{(iii)},
obtaining (7):
$$\llrr{\Gamma, x: A, x_\mf{set}: \tset{A}}, \Delta, x_\ell: \llrr{A}
\semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}
: A_\mf{res}$$
\item Finally, we apply \thref{thm:deltaflag} (flag-typing under $\delta$)
to premises \emph{(iii)} and \emph{(iv)}
we get (8) that $\Delta, \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \delta_\I(p): \tref{\tbool}$.
\item We can now derive the conclusion,
expanding definitions and
weakening by \hl{REF} along the way:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ref] {
  \inferrule*[right=(3)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \llrr{\Delta} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \llrr{v}_\I: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \llrr{\Delta} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \eref{\llrr{v}}: \tref{\llrr{A}}
  \quad (9)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Get] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
} {
  x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \eget{x_\ell}: \llrr{A}
  \quad (10)
}
\and
\inferrule*{
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v x_\ell : \tref{\llrr{A}}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Var] { } {
    y: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v y: \llrr{A}
  }
} {
  x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, y: \llrr{A} \vdash_e \eset{x_\ell}{y}: \tone
  \quad (11)
  \qquad (\ts{\small{TE-Set}})
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  \inferrule*[right=(8)] { } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v \delta_\I(p) : \tref{\tbool}
  }
  \
  \inferrule*[right=TV-True] { } {
    \Delta \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_v \mf{true}: \tbool
  }
} {
  \Delta, \vdash_e \eset{\delta_\I(p)}{\mf{true}}: \tone
  \quad (12)
}
\and
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
    \inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
      \inferrule* { } {(11)}
      \and
      \inferrule* { } {(12)}
    } {
      \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}}, y: \llrr{A} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
      \vdash_v
      \eset{x_\ell}{y} 
      \semi \eset{\delta_\I(p)}{\mf{true}}: \tone
    }
  } {
    \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v
    \lambda y.\cdots: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  }
} {
  \Delta, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \eret{\lambda y.\cdots}: \llrr{A} \rightarrow \tone
  \quad (13)
  \qquad (\ts{\small{TE-Ret}})
}
\end{mathpar}
