All rules in the final derivation below are \ts{TE-Let}:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule* {
  \inferrule* { } {(9)}
  \
  \inferrule* {
    \inferrule* { } {(10)}
    \
    \inferrule* {
      \inferrule* { } {(13)}
      \and
      \inferrule* { } {(7)}
    } {
      \llrr{\Gamma}, \llrr{\Delta}, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}},
      x: \llrr{A}
      \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e
      \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\cdots}{\cdots}
      : A_\mf{ret}
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma}, \llrr{\Delta}, x_\ell: \tref{\llrr{A}} 
    \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e
    \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\cdots}
    : A_\mf{ret}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma}, \llrr{\Delta} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}_\I}}{\cdots}
  : A_\mf{ret}
}
\end{mathpar}\
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, bodies, initial.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell_0$
(where $\Ell \subseteq \Ell_0$),
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$, and
\item $(\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell) \semi \Pi \vDash \delta: \Delta$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, bodies, static.]
\label{thm:tprezbodt}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I: A_\mf{res}$.

\begin{Proof}\
\begin{itemize}
\item For any $\hat{e}$, we have that
$\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top = \elet{x_f}{\eref{\mf{false}}}
{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/p}_{\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}}$
(for arbitrary $p$).
\item We want to apply \thref{thm:tprezbodi}
(body type preservation at $\phi=\mf{Init}$):
\begin{itemize}
  \item Since $\hat{e}=\hat{e}_0$, we have that $\Ell=\Ell_0$,
  i.e. $\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell = \emptyset$.
  \item Then by definition, $\Delta=x_f: \tref{\tbool}$
  satisfies the condition (1) that
  $(\Ell_0 \setminus \Ell) \semi \Pi \vDash
  \mf{flag} \mapsto x_f: \Delta$.
  \item By \thref{thm:tprezbodi} on (1) and both premises,
  \emph{(i)} and \emph{(ii)},
  we get (2) that
  $\llrr{\Gamma}, x_f: \tref{\tbool} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/p}
  _{\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}: A_\mf{res}$.
\end{itemize}
\item We derive the conclusion as follows:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule*[right=TE-Ref] {
    \inferrule*[right=TV-True] { } {
      \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
      \vdash_e
      \mf{false}: \tbool
    }
  } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_e
    \eref{\mf{false}}: \tref{\tbool}
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* { } {(2)}
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_e
  \elet{x_f}{\eref{\mf{false}}}
  {\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/p}_{\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}}:
  A_\mf{res}
}
\end{mathpar}\
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, bodies, static.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\hat{e}}^\top_\I$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, runtime values.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash u: A$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{u}_\I: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction on the derivation of the typing premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Cases} \ts{TR-Var}, \ts{TR-Unit}, \ts{TR-Nil}, \ts{TR-Cons}:
Elided; should be trivial, but should fill in anyways.

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Lit}, \ts{TR-Node}:
Also easy and elided, but should fill in\\
\hl{once we can do ID}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-Cell}: We have
$\llrr{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
= \mf{cell}(\lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\emptyset,
\I(p), \llrr{u})$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-Cell] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Pi[p]
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash u: \ttree
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash \mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, we have (3) that 
$\Pi[p] \semi \Pi \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset$.
\item By \thref{thm:tprezbods} (type preservation on bodies
at $\phi=\mf{Succ}$) on (1), (3),
and premise \emph{(ii)}, we get (4) that
$\cdot \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vdash_e 
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset}: \ttree$.
\item Also from (1), we know $p \in \dom(\Pi)$,
so by premise \emph{(ii)} we get that
$\I(p)$ is well-defined.
\item Then by definition, (5) $\I(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}$.
\item By inductive hypothesis on (2) and premise \emph{(ii)},
we get (6) $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_v \llrr{u}_\I: \ttree$.
\item We conclude by derivation as follows,
weakening (4) by $\llrr{\Gamma}$ according to \hl{REF}:
\end{itemize}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \inferrule*[right=(4)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_e
    \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset}:
    \ttree
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_v
  \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset}:
  \tone \rightarrow \ttree
  \quad (7)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \inferrule*[right=(5)] { } {
    \I(p): \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_v
  \I(p):
  \tref{\tbool}
  \quad (8)
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cell] {
  \inferrule* { } {(7)}
  \and
  \inferrule* { } {(8)}
  \and
  \inferrule*[right=(6)] { } {
    \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
    \vdash_v \llrr{u}_\I: \ttree
  }
} {
  \llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
  \vdash_v
  \mf{cell}(
    \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset},
    \I(p),
    \llrr{u}
  ): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}\
\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, runtime values.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash u: A$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{u}_\I$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, runtime expressions.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \rho: A$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\Gamma}, \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e \llrr{\rho}_\I: \llrr{A}$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction over the derivation of the typing premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalI}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}}
&= \elet{\mf{view, flag, spec}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_\emptyset}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{\\
    &\quad\ \eret{\mf{cell(spec, flag, tree)}}
  }
}
\end{align*}
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalI] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell \cup \Pi[p]
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}: \tview
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, (3) $\Pi[p] \semi \Pi \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset$.
\item \thref{thm:tprezbodi} (body type preservation at $\phi=\mf{Init}$)
on (1-3) and premise \emph{(ii)}
gives us (4) that $\llrr{\Gamma}\semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}: A_\mf{res}$.
\item \hl{continue...}
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{TR-EvalS}: We have
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}}
&= \elet{\mf{view}}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}}_p}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{\mf{tree}}{\mf{init}\ \mf{view}}{\\
    &\quad\ \eret{\mf{cell}(
      \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\emptyset,
      \I(p),
      \mf{tree}
    )}
  }
}\\
\end{align*}
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TR-EvalS] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Pi[p]
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \cdot \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {\Ell \subseteq \Pi[p]}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \Vdash_\rho \hat{e}_0 \gg^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By definition, (3) gives us (4)
that $\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset$.
\item \thref{thm:tprezbods} (body type preservation at $\phi=\mf{Succ}$)
on (2), (4), and premise \emph{(ii)}
gives us (5) that $\llrr{\Gamma}\semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset}: \tview$.
\item By definition, we know (6) that
$\Ell \semi \Pi \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset$.
\item We again apply \thref{thm:tprezbods},
this time to (1), (6), and premise \emph{(ii)},
to get (7) that $\llrr{\Gamma}\semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I
\vdash_e
\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset}: \tview$.
\item \hl{continue...}
\end{itemize}

\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, runtime expressions.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \rho: A$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{\rho}_\I$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\begin{lemma}[Type preservation, memory.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\Gamma \semi \llrr{\Pi}_\I \vDash \llrr{m}_\I$.

\begin{Proof}
Consider some arbitrary $p \in m$:
\begin{itemize}
\item By premise \emph{(i)}, we have that $p \in \Pi$.
(Note that, by premise \emph{(ii)}, $\I(p)$ is well-defined.)
\item By definition, we have (1) that 
$\I(p) \mapsto \llrr{m[p].\mf{rerender}} \in \llrr{m}_\I$.
\item Also by definition, (2)
$\I(p) \mapsto \tbool \in \llrr{\Pi}_\I$.
\item We want to show $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
\llrr{m}_\I[\I(p)] : \llrr{\Pi}_\I[\I(p)]$.
\item By (1) and (2),
it suffices to show $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
\llrr{m[p].\mf{rerender}} : \tbool$.
\item This is given by \hl{REF} (rerender-typing).
\end{itemize}

Now consider some arbitrary $\ell \in m[p].\mf{st}$:
\begin{itemize}
\item By premise \emph{(i)}, we have that $\ell \in \Pi[p]$.
(Note that, by premise \emph{(ii)}, $\I(p, \ell)$ is well-defined.)
\item By definition, we have (3) that 
$\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]}_\I \in \llrr{m}_\I$.
\item Also by definition, (4)
$\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{\Pi[p][\ell]} \in \llrr{\Pi}_\I$.
\item We want to show $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
\llrr{m}_\I[\I(p, \ell)] : \llrr{\Pi}_\I[\I(p, \ell)]$.
\item By (3) and (4),
it suffices to show $\llrr{\Gamma} \semi \llrr{\Pi} \vdash_v
\llrr{m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]}_\I : \llrr{\Pi[p][\ell]}$.
\item Premise \emph{(i)} tells us that
$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]: \Pi[p][\ell]$.
The conclusion follows from \thref{thm:tprezval}
(value type preservation under translation).
\end{itemize}

\end{Proof}
\end{lemma}

\begin{corollary}[Well-definedness, memory.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$ and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{m}_\I$ is well-defined.
\end{corollary}

\subsection{Simulation}

Define $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \I \vDash \pair{m}{e}$
to hold when:
\begin{itemize}
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A \mid E$, and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$.
\end{itemize}
(We define analogous notions for bodies $\hat{e}$ and runtime terms $\rho$
as well.)

\begin{theorem}[Simulation, expressions.]
\label{thm:simexp}
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'$ and
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \I \vDash \pair{m}{e}$,
\end{enumerate}
then $\llrr{m}_\I \semi \llrr{e}_\I 
\mapsto^+ \llrr{e'}_\I \semi \llrr{m'}_\I$.

\begin{Proof}
By induction over the derivation of the step judgment \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{E-Let}: We have 
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}}=\elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}}$
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=E-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e_1}{e_1'}{m'}}
} {
  \estep{m}{\elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}}{\elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}}{m'}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: \ptp_1 \mid E}
  \and
  \Gamma, x: \ptp_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: \ptp_2 \mid E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: \ptp_2 \mid E
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (2) and definition of premise \emph{(ii)},
we have (3) that $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \I \vDash \pair{m}{e_1}$.
\item By inductive hypothesis on (1) and (3),
we get (4) that $\llrr{m}_\I \semi \llrr{e_1}_\I 
\mapsto^+ \llrr{e_1'}_\I \semi \llrr{m'}_\I$.
\item By LEMMA (transitivity under $\mf{let}$) on (4),
we conclude that $\elet{x}{\llrr{e_1}}{\llrr{e_2}} \mapsto^+
\elet{x}{\llrr{e_1'}}{\llrr{e_2}}$, 
which equals $\llrr{\elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}}$
and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-Ret}: \begin{itemize}
\item We have
$\llrr{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}{e}} = \elet{x}{\eret{\llrr{v}}}{\llrr{e}}$
and $m \semi \elet{x}{\eret{v}}{e} \mapsto e[v/x] \semi m$.
\item In the target language, we get that
$\llrr{m} \semi \elet{x}{\eret{\llrr{v}}}{\llrr{e}}
\mapsto
\llrr{e}[\llrr{v}/x] \semi \llrr{m}$.
\item It suffices to show that $\llrr{e[v/x]} = \llrr{e}[\llrr{v}/x]$.
This is given by LEMMA (substitution), and we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-AppL}: We have 
$m \semi (\lambda x.e)\ v \mapsto e[v/x] \semi m$.
\begin{itemize}
\item The translation steps as follows:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{m} \semi \llrr{(\lambda x.e)\ v}
&= \llrr{m} \semi \lambda x.\llrr{e}\ \llrr{v}\\
&\mapsto \llrr{m} \semi \llrr{e}[\llrr{v}/x]
\end{align*}
\item The conclusion again follows from LEMMA (substitution).
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{E-AppS}: We have that
$m \semi \vset{\ell}{p}\ v \mapsto
\eret{()} \semi m'$,
where $m'=m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell]=v\ 
\mf{and}\ m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt$,
and:
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-AppS] {
  \inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
    \inferrule* {(1)} {\ell: \ptp \in \Pi[p]}
  } {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: \tset{\ptp}
  }
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ptp
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}\ v: \tone \mid \emut
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{itemize}
\item By (1) and premise \emph{(ii)},
$\I(p, \ell)$ is a well-defined location in the target language.
($\I(p)$ is similarly well-defined.)
\item Therefore, the following translation is well-defined:
$$\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}\ v} = (\lambda x. \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x}
\semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}})\ \llrr{v}$$
\item The translation steps as follows:
\begin{align*}
&\llrr{m} \semi (\lambda x. \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{x}
\semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}})\ \llrr{v}\\
&\mapsto \llrr{m} \semi \eset{\I(p, \ell)}{\llrr{v}}
\semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}}\\
&\mapsto^+ \llrr{m}[\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{v}]
\semi \eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}}\\
&\mapsto \llrr{m}[\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{v}, \I(p) \mapsto \mf{true}]
\semi \eret{()}
\end{align*}
\item Trivially $\llrr{\eret{()}}=\eret{()}$;
it remains to be shown that
$\llrr{m'} = \llrr{m}[\I(p, \ell) \mapsto \llrr{v}, \I(p) \mapsto \mf{true}]$.
\item This follows by definition of $\llrr{m'}$,
and we are done.\\(\hl{TODO: does this need a lemma?})
\end{itemize}

\tb{Cases} \ts{E-CaseN}, \ts{E-CaseC}: Trivial, but should fill in.
\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[Simulation, bodies, initial phase.]
If \begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $m \semi \hat{e} \mapsto^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}' \semi m'$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \I \vDash \pair{m}{\hat{e}}$, and
\item $p \in \dom\ \Pi$,
\end{enumerate}
then there exist some $\Pi' \rpo \Pi$ and $\I' \rpo \I$ such that
\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item $\llrr{m}_\I \semi \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset} 
\mapsto^+ \llrr{\hat{e}'}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I'/\emptyset} 
\semi \llrr{m'}_{\I'}$ and
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi' \semi \I' \vDash \pair{m'}{\hat{e}'}$.
\end{enumerate}

\begin{Proof}
By premise \emph{(ii)}, we have the following:
\begin{enumerate}[label=(ii-\alph*)]
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$,
\item $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$, and
\item $\Pi \vDash \I$.
\end{enumerate}
(We refer to the sub-conclusions of conclusion \emph{(ii)} in the same way.)

Unless otherwise mentioned, we choose $\I'=\I$ and $\Pi'=\Pi$,
such that conclusion \emph{(ii)}
is a corollary of (\hl{THEOREM}) type preservation
applied to premises \emph{(i)}, (ii-a), and (ii-b).
\hl{TODO}: not true, tprez only gives us $\Pi' \rpo \Pi$
(wait: is this true?)

We proceed by induction on the derivation 
of the small-step premise \emph{(i)}.

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Lift}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{e}{\mf{Init}}{p}{e'}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview \mid \epsilon
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\end{mathpar}
and:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}
&= \elet{x_r}{\llrr{e}}{\\
  &\quad\ \eret{(x_r,\I(p),\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}
  ^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset})}
}
\end{align*}
\begin{itemize}
\item Premise \emph{(iii)} alongside (ii-c)
gives us that $\I(p)$ is well-defined,
i.e. the translation above is well-defined at $p$.
\item By \thref{thm:simexp} (expression simulation) on (1)
and premise \emph{(ii)},
we have (2) that $\llrr{m}_\I \semi \llrr{e}_\I 
\mapsto^+ \llrr{e'}_\I \semi \llrr{m}_\I$.
\item By LEMMA (transitivity under $\mf{let}$) on (2),
we get $m \semi \elet{x}{\llrr{e}}{e_2}
\mapsto^+ \elet{x}{\llrr{e'}}{e_2} \semi m$,
which equals $\llrr{e'}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}$
and so we are done.
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Let}: We have
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(1)} {\estep{m}{e}{e'}{m}}
} {
  \bstep{m}{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}{\phi}{p}{\elet{x}{e'}{\hat{e}}}{m}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \inferrule* {(2)} {
    \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \ptp \mid \epsilon
  }
  \and
  \inferrule* {(3)} {
    \Gamma, x: \ptp \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  }
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}
and
$\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}
= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}}$.
\begin{itemize}
\item By \thref{thm:simexp} (expression simulation) on (1),
(2),
and premises \emph{(ii-b)} and \emph{(ii-c)},
we have (4) that $\llrr{m}_\I \semi \llrr{e}_\I 
\mapsto^+ \llrr{e'}_\I \semi \llrr{m}_\I$
and (5) that $\Gamma \semi \Pi \semi \I \vDash \pair{m}{e}$.
\item By LEMMA (transitivity under $\mf{let}$) on (4),
we obtain that 
$m \semi \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}
{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}}
\mapsto^+
\elet{x}{\llrr{e'}_\I}
{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}}
\semi m'$, and we are done.
\item \hl{TODO}: demonstrate typing conclusion, but should be easy
\end{itemize}

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-Ret}: We have
$m \semi \elet{x}{\eret{v}}{\hat{e}} \mapsto \hat{e}[v/x]$.

The translation steps as follows:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{m} \semi \llrr{\elet{x}{\eret{v}}
\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}
&=
\llrr{m} \semi \elet{x}{\eret{\llrr{v}}}
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}\\
&\mapsto \llrr{m} \semi
\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}[\llrr{v}/x]
\end{align*}
The conclusion follows from LEMMA (substitution)
and we are done.

\tb{Case} \ts{EB-StI}:

\end{Proof}
\end{theorem}

Goals:
\begin{itemize}
\item type soundness of source
\item soundness of target?
\item translation well-definedness
\item translation type preservation
\item translation simulation
\item translation reflection?
\end{itemize}

\section{Custom Hooks}

\subsection{Motivation}

Using only React's built-in \texttt{useState},
we are limited to placing individual state cells 
in component bodies, e.g.:

\begin{lstlisting}
let foo = comp {
  let x, xset = useState(0) in
  let y, yset = useState("foo") in
  ...
}
\end{lstlisting}

However, often we want some logic surrounding
our primitive state: the most common usage in React
is for \emph{query caching}.
For a simpler example, \texttt{useNat}
ensures that the state value (an integer)
is greater than zero:

\begin{lstlisting}
const useNat = (n: int) => {
  const n' = (n >= 0) ? n : 0;
  let [val, setVal] = useState(n');
  const setter = (newVal: int) => {
    if (newVal >= 0) {
      setVal(newVal);
    }
  };
  return [val, setter];
}
\end{lstlisting}

\texttt{useNat} is a \emph{custom hook}:
it can be used just like \texttt{useState}
at the top-level of component bodies
(or other custom hooks!),
and any hooks in \emph{its} body
must occur at the top-level.

My language currently does not support
the \emph{composition of hooks} in this way—
and I would like it to!

\subsection{Hooks as Modes}

Insight: composing hooks can be done in \emph{monadic style},
which enforces an order (and a top-level requirement)
on its constitutent parts.

Hooks are assigned the \emph{modal type} $\mf{Hook}\ A$,
and their evaluation must be sequenced with the $\mf{use}$ construct 
(i.e. ``bind'').

Components are the special case of hooks with $A=\mf{View}$,
and can be injected into other views via the $\mf{spec}$ constructor.

(Ignoring most runtime details for the moment.)

\subsubsection{Syntax}

We let $\mf{return}$ refer to the ``monadic'' return,
whereas $\mf{val}$ refers to a ``pure'' return.

(We need product types/constructs too, but these are easy.)

\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s 
\mid \lambda x.e \mid [] \mid v :: v
\mid \mhl{\mf{hook}\ e} \mid \mhl{\mf{state}}\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v} \mid \mhl{\mf{spec}\ v}
\mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v}\\
\text{Expression } e &::= \mf{val}\ v \mid \mhl{\eret{v}} \mid \mhl{\elet{x}{e}{e}} 
\mid \mhl{\elet{x}{\mf{use}^\ell\ e}{e}}\\
&\quad\mid v\ v \mid
\ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \rightarrow \ptp
\mid \mf{List}\ A \mid \tview \mid \tattr \mid \mhl{\mf{Hook}\ A}
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Typing}

Value judgment: $\Gamma \vdash_v v: A$

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  x: A \in \Gamma
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v x: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash_e e: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \lambda x.e: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v []: \mf{List}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: A
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \mf{List}\ A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1 :: v_2: \mf{List}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\hl{TV-Hook}] {
  \Gamma \vdash_h e: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \mf{hook}\ e: \mf{Hook}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\hl{TV-State}] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash \mf{state}: A \rightarrow 
  \mf{Hook}\ (A \times (A \rightarrow \tone))
}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Text] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vtxt{v}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Tag] {
  \Gamma \vdash v_1: \mf{List}\ \tattr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash v_2: \mf{List}\ \tview
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vtag{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\hl{TV-Spec}] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: \mf{Hook}\ \tview
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \mf{spec}\ v: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Attr] {
  \Gamma \vdash v_1: \tstr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash v_2: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vattr{v_1}{v_2}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Click] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: \tone \rightarrow \tone
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vclick{v}: \tattr
}
\end{mathpar}

Pure expression judgment: $\Gamma \vdash_e e: A$

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Val] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \mf{val}\ v: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \Gamma \vdash_e e_1: A_1
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash_e e_2: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \Gamma \vdash v_1: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash v_2: A_1
} {
  \Gamma \vdash v_1\ v_2: A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Case] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: \mf{List}\ A_1
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash e_1: A_2
  \\
  \Gamma, x: A_1, xs: \mf{List}\ A_1 \vdash e_2: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x :: xs}{e_2}: A_2
}
\end{mathpar}

Hook judgment: $\Gamma \vdash_h e: A$

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TH-Ret] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_h \eret{v}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\hl{TM-Use}] {
  \Gamma \vdash_e e_1: \mf{Hook}\ A_1
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash_h e: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_h \elet{x}{\mf{use}\ e_1}{e_2}: A_2
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

Pure expression judgment: $e \mapsto e'$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EE-Let] {
  e_1 \mapsto e_1'
} {
  \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2} \mapsto \elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\elet{x}{\mf{val}\ v}{e} &\mapsto e[v/x] &&\textsc{EE-Val}\\
(\lambda x.e)\ v &\mapsto e[v/x] &&\textsc{EE-App}\\
\ecase{[]}{[]}{e_1}{x :: xs}{e_2}
&\mapsto e_1 &&\textsc{EE-CaseN}\\
\ecase{v :: vs}{[]}{e_1}{x :: xs}{e_2}
&\mapsto e_2[v/x, vs/xs] &&\textsc{EE-CaseC}\\
\end{align*}

Hook judgment: $m \semi e \hookrightarrow^{\phi/p}_\Ell e' \semi m'$
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EH-Ret] {
  e \mapsto e'
} {
  m \semi \eret{e}
  \hookrightarrow^{\phi/p}_\Ell
  \eret{e'} \semi m
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=EH-Use] {
  m \semi e_1 \hookrightarrow^{\phi/p}_{\Ell,\ell} e_1' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \elet{x}{\mf{use}^\ell\ e_1}{e_2} 
  \hookrightarrow^{\phi/p}_\Ell
  \elet{x}{\mf{use}^\ell\ e_1'}{e_2} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \elet{x}{\mf{use}^\ell\ \eret{\mf{val}\ v}}{e}
&\hookrightarrow^{\phi/p}_\Ell
e[v/x] \semi m &&\textsc{EH-Val}\\
m \semi \mf{state}\ v
&\hookrightarrow^{\mf{Init}/p}_\Ell
\eret{\mf{val}\ v} \semi m'
&&\textsc{EH-StI}\\
&(m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\Ell] = v)\\
m \semi \mf{state}\ v
&\hookrightarrow^{\mf{Succ}/p}_\Ell
\eret{\mf{val}\ v'} \semi m
&&\textsc{EH-StS}\\
&(v' = m[p].\mf{st}[\Ell])\\
\end{align*}
(state rules are missing setters for the moment,
and I'm omitting a bunch of details re: memory syntax,
but should be pretty straightforward?)

A program has type $\mf{View}$.

\subsubsection{Example}

Allowing ourselves integers and conditionals:

\begin{lstlisting}[mathescape]
let nat = $\lambda$x.val hook {
  let y, yset = use state x in
  let setter = return val $\lambda$z.
    if z >= 0
      then yset z
      else val ()
    in
  return val (y, setter)
} 
in
spec hook {
  let n, nset = use nat 5 in
  return val (
    <div onClick={$\lambda$().nset(n-1)}>
      {n}
    </div>
  )
}
\end{lstlisting}

$\mf{nat}$ has type $\mf{Int} \rightarrow 
\mf{Hook}\ (\mf{Int} \times (\mf{Int} \rightarrow \tone))$.

\subsection{A Problem}

Consider a similar language with
a single monadic action $\mf{tick}: \bigcirc \tone$:

\begin{lstlisting}[mathescape]
let x = use return (
  if v < 0
    then state 0
    else return (0, $\lambda x.()$)
) in
let y = use x in return y
\end{lstlisting}

Evaluation of the above term may or may not cause a tick,
depending on the value of $v$.

We no longer have the needed guarantee that
actions will be executed in the same order every time.

The problem (I think?) only arises when
a double-modality $\bigcirc\bigcirc$ arises somewhere in the
typing derivation: above, the expression bound to $x$
has type $\bigcirc\bigcirc\tone$.

Maybe we can statically forbid this somehow?

The problem (or another similar one) might stem from allowing
\emph{hooks as first-class values}:
\begin{lstlisting}[mathescape]
let x = if rand()
  then hook1
  else hook2
in let y = use x in...
\end{lstlisting}
If hooks are values, then we can certainly conditionalize
their execution as above.

Idea: hooks as ``procedures'' (second-class values)?

\subsection{Effects All The Way}

Idea: track state as an effect,
remove the syntactic restriction.

(Goal is to implicitly address Simon's question:
is the syntactic restriction in React \emph{necessary},
or an artifact of JavaScript not having an effect system?)

\begin{align*}
\text{Value } v &::= x \mid () \mid s 
\mid \lambda x.e \mid [] \mid v :: v \mid \mhl{\mf{state}}\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v} \mid \mhl{\mf{spec}\ e}
\mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v}\\
\text{Expression } e &::= \eret{v} \mid \elet{x}{e}{e} \mid v\ v\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x_\mf{hd} :: x_\mf{tl}}{e_2}\\
\text{Type } \ptp &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \ptp \arrow{E} \ptp
\mid \mf{List}\ A \mid \tview \mid \tattr\\
\text{Effect } E &::= \epsilon \mid \mf{Mut} \mid \mhl{\mf{St}\ \Ell}\\
\text{Type list } \Ell &::= A \mid \Ell, A
\end{align*}

Effect composition $E_1 \circ E_2$ is defined as follows:
\begin{align*}
\epsilon \circ \epsilon &= \epsilon\\
\epsilon \circ \mf{Mut} = \mf{Mut} \circ \epsilon &= \mf{Mut}\\
\epsilon \circ \mf{St}\ \Ell = \mf{St}\ \Ell \circ \epsilon &= \mf{St}\ \Ell\\
\mf{St}\ \Ell_1 \circ \mf{St}\ \Ell_2 &= \mf{St}\ \Ell_1 \circ \Ell_2\\
\mf{Mut} \circ \mf{St}\ \Ell = \mf{St}\ \Ell \circ \mf{Mut} &= \bot
\end{align*}

Type list composition $\Ell_1 \circ \Ell_2$ is list concatenation.

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  x: A \in \Gamma
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v x: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash_e e: A_2 \bang E
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \lambda x.e: A_1 \arrow{E} A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v []: \mf{List}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: A
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \mf{List}\ A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1 :: v_2: \mf{List}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\hl{TV-State}] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash \mf{state}: A \arrow{\mf{St}\: A} 
  \mf{Hook}\ (A \times (A \arrow{\mf{Mut}} \tone))
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Text] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vtxt{v}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Tag] {
  \Gamma \vdash v_1: \mf{List}\ \tattr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash v_2: \mf{List}\ \tview
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vtag{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=\hl{TV-Spec}] {
  \Gamma \vdash e: \tview \bang \mf{St}\ \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \mf{spec}\ e: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Attr] {
  \Gamma \vdash v_1: \tstr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash v_2: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vattr{v_1}{v_2}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Click] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: \tone \rightarrow \tone
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \vclick{v}: \tattr
}
\end{mathpar}

\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ret] {
  \Gamma \vdash v: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash e: A \bang \epsilon
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \Gamma \vdash e_1: A_1 \bang E_1
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash e_2: A_2 \bang E_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: A_2 \bang E_1 \circ E_2
}
\end{mathpar}

\emph{Issue}: how do we define setters
to refer to a specific location?

If we have a conditional hook:
\begin{lstlisting}[mathescape]
let x = if rand()
  then hook1
  else hook2
in ...
\end{lstlisting}
Say we obtain a setter from the first hook:
we should be able to invoke the setter,
regardless of which hook is currently active...
but the previous approach of syntactic labelling doesn't work,
since the labels will be different between hooks!

Possible approach: replace labels with \emph{indices},
generated at runtime (via a counter on the step judgment
tracking how many cells have been initialized)?

Relies on observation that, even if a different
(syntactic) state cell is being referred to,
it must have the same type (and so it makes sense to set it).

Problem with this approach: maybe the syntactic distinction
is good, actually? Conditionalizing (maybe very complicated)
custom hooks seems confusing?
(And might make the translation break/become more complicated?)

\subsection{Hooks as Macros}

$\cdots$

\section{Language Hierarchy}

Solid arrows denote extensions and dotted arrows denote translations.

\begin{center}
\begin{forest}
  for tree={
    grow'=north,
    edge={-Stealth},
    l sep=20mm,
    s sep=20mm
  }
  [$\lambda_\mf{view}$
    [$\lambda_\mf{dyn}$, name=dyn
      [$\lambda^\mf{hook}_\mf{dyn}$, name=hook]
      [$\lambda^\mf{ref}_\mf{dyn}$, name=ref]
    ]
    [$\lambda_{\mf{view}+}$, name=viewplus
      [$\lambda^\mf{ref}_{\mf{view}+}$, name=refplus]
    ]
  ]
  \draw[dotted, -Stealth] (hook) to (ref);
  \draw[dotted, -Stealth] (ref)  to (refplus);
  \draw[dotted, -Stealth] (dyn)  to (viewplus);
\end{forest}\\
\end{center}
Broadly:
\begin{itemize}
\item $\lambda_\mf{view}$ extends STLC
with some basic constructs (pairs, lists, booleans)
and HTML constructs (text, tags, attributes).
\begin{itemize}
  \item We should relate this to one of the document calculi of
  Crichton and Krishnamurthi (2024).
  (Maybe $\mathcal{D}^\mf{Article}_\mf{TProg}$?)
\end{itemize}
\item $\lambda_\mf{dyn}$ extends $\lambda_\mf{view}$
with a ``reactive semantics'', comprising
a language of runtime terms (trees, initialization, updating, handling).
\begin{itemize}
  \item Since there is no state at this level,
  ``reactions'' simply execute handlers (and any side effects within).
\end{itemize}
\item $\lambda^\mf{hook}_\mf{dyn}$ extends $\lambda_\mf{dyn}$ with
React-style stateful components.
\item $\lambda^\mf{ref}_\mf{dyn}$ extends $\lambda_\mf{dyn}$ with
general references and associated view/tree constructors.
\item $\lambda_{\mf{view}+}$ extends $\lambda_\mf{view}$ with
general recursion, tree constructs, and a thin reactive layer.
\begin{itemize}
  \item These elements are sufficient to encode the full reactive semantics
  of $\lambda_\mf{dyn}$.
\end{itemize}
\item $\lambda^\mf{ref}_{\mf{view}+}$ extends $\lambda_{\mf{view}+}$ with
general references and associated view/tree constructors.
\end{itemize}

\subsection{$\lambda_\mf{view}$}

\subsubsection{Syntax}
\begin{align*}
\text{Values } v &::= x \mid () \mid s \mid \lambda x.e \mid (v, v)
\mid \mf{true} \mid \mf{false} \mid [] \mid v :: v\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{v} \mid \vtag{v}{v} \mid \vattr{v}{v} \mid \vclick{v}\\
\text{Computations } e &::= \mf{val}\ v \mid \elet{x}{e}{e} \mid v\ v
\mid \eif{v}{e}{e} \mid \efst{v} \mid \esnd{v}\\
\text{Types } A &::= \tone \mid \tstr \mid \tbool \mid A \rightarrow A
\mid A \times A \mid \mf{List}\ A \mid \tview \mid \tattr\\
\text{Type context } \Gamma &::= \emptyset \mid \Gamma, x: A
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Type System}
\fbox{$\Gamma \vdash_v v: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Var] {
  x: A \in \Gamma
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v x: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Unit] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v (): \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Str] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v s: \tstr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-True] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \mf{true}: \tbool
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-False] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \mf{false}: \tbool
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v []: \mf{List}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cons] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: A
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v vs: \mf{List}\ A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v :: vs: \mf{List}\ A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash_e e: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \lambda x.e: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Pair] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: A_1
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v (v_1, v_2): A_1 \times A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Text] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \vtxt{v}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Tag] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: \mf{List}\ \tattr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \mf{List}\ \tview
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \vtag{v_1}{v_2}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Attr] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: \tstr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \vattr{v_1}{v_2}: \tattr
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Click] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tone \rightarrow \tone
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \vclick{v_1}: \tattr
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \vdash_e e: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Val] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \mf{val}\ v: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \Gamma \vdash_e e_1: A_1
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \vdash_e e_2: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: A_1
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e v_1\ v_2: A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-If] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tbool
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_e e_1: A
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_e e_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \eif{v}{e_1}{e_2}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Fst] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: A_1 \times A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \efst{v}: A_1
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Snd] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: A_1 \times A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \esnd{v}: A_2
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

\fbox{$e \mapsto e'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EE-Let] {
  e_1 \mapsto e_1'
} {
  \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2} \mapsto \elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2}
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\elet{x}{\mf{val}\ v}{e} &\mapsto e[v/x] &&\textsc{EE-Val}\\
(\lambda x.e)\ v &\mapsto e[v/x] &&\textsc{EE-App}\\
\eif{\mf{true}}{e_1}{e_2} &\mapsto e_1 &&\textsc{EE-IfT}\\
\eif{\mf{false}}{e_1}{e_2} &\mapsto e_2 &&\textsc{EE-IfF}\\
\efst{(v_1, v_2)} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ v_1 &&\textsc{EE-Fst}\\
\esnd{(v_1, v_2)} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ v_2 &&\textsc{EE-Snd}\\
\end{align*}

\subsection{$\lambda_\mf{dyn}$}

Extends $\lambda_\mf{view}$.

\subsubsection{Syntax}
All new syntax is ``runtime-only'':
\begin{align*}
v &::= \cdots \mid \elit{v} \mid \enode{id}{v}{u}\\
e &::= \cdots \mid \einit{v} \mid \einits{v}
\mid \esearch{id}{u} \mid \esearches{id}{u}\\
&\quad\mid \echk{u} \mid \echks{u}
\mid \escan{v} \mid \escans{v}\\
A &::= \cdots \mid \ttree
\end{align*}

Given a well-typed user-level program $\cdot \vdash_e e: \tview$,
the initial configuration is $\elet{x}{e}{\einit{x}}$.

\subsubsection{Type System}

\fbox{$\Gamma \vdash_v v: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lit] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \elit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Node] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: \mf{List}\ \tattr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \mf{List}\ \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \enode{id}{v_1}{v_2}: \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \vdash_e e: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Init] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tview
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \einit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Inits] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \mf{List}\ \tview
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \einits{v}: \mf{List}\ \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Search] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \esearch{id}{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Searches] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \mf{List}\ \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \esearches{id}{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Scan] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tattr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \escan{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Inits] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \mf{List}\ \tattr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \escans{v}: \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Check] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \echk{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TR-Checks] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \mf{List}\ \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \echks{v}: \mf{List}\ \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

\fbox{$e \mapsto e'$}

Rendering rules:
\begin{align*}
\einit{\vtxt{v}} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \elit{v} && \textsc{EE-InitText}\\
\einit{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}} &\mapsto \elet{x}{\einits{v_2}}{
  \mf{val}\ \enode{id}{v_1}{x}
}
&& \textsc{EE-InitTag}\\
&\quad (\mf{id}\ \text{fresh})\\
\echk{\elit{v}} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \elit{v} && \textsc{ER-CheckLit}\\
\echk{\enode{id}{v}{u}} &\mapsto \elet{x}{\echks{u}}{
  \mf{val}\ \enode{id}{v}{x}
}
&& \textsc{ER-CheckNode}
\end{align*}

Event handling rules:
\begin{align*}
\esearch{id}{\elit{v}} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () && \textsc{ER-SearchLit}\\
\esearch{id}{\enode{id}{v}{u}} &\mapsto 
\elet{()}{\escans{v}}{\esearch{id}{u}} && \textsc{ER-SearchNodeT}\\
\esearch{id}{\enode{id'}{v}{u}} &\mapsto \esearch{id}{u} \quad
(\mf{id} \neq \mf{id}')
&& \textsc{ER-SearchNodeF}\\
\escan{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () && \textsc{ER-ScanAttr}\\
\escan{\vclick{v}} &\mapsto v () && \textsc{ER-ScanClick}
\end{align*}

List rules:
\begin{align*}
\einits{[]} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ [] && \textsc{EE-InitsNil}\\
\einits{v :: vs} &\mapsto \elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{xs}{\einits{vs}}{
    \mf{val}\ x :: xs
  }
} && \textsc{EE-InitsCons}\\
\echks{[]} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () && \textsc{EE-ChecksNil}\\
\echks{v :: vs} &\mapsto \elet{x}{\echk{v}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{xs}{\echks{vs}}{
    \mf{val}\ x :: xs
  }
} && \textsc{EE-ChecksCons}\\
\esearches{id}{[]} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () && \textsc{EE-SearchesNil}\\
\esearches{id}{v :: vs} &\mapsto \elet{()}{\esearch{id}{v}}{\esearches{id}{vs}} 
&& \textsc{EE-SearchesCons}\\
\escans{[]} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () && \textsc{EE-ScansNil}\\
\escans{v :: vs} &\mapsto \elet{()}{\escan{v}}{\escan{vs}} 
&& \textsc{EE-ScansCons}
\end{align*}

\fbox{$e \mapsto_0 e'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EZ-Top] {
  e \mapsto e'
} {
  e \mapsto_0 e'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\mf{val}\ v &\mapsto_0 \elet{()}{\esearch{id}{v}}{\echk{v}}
&&\textsc{EZ-Event}
\end{align*}

\subsection{$\lambda^\mf{hook}_\mf{dyn}$}

Extends $\lambda_\mf{dyn}$.

\subsubsection{Syntax}

Greyed terms are runtime-only.
\begin{align*}
v &::= \cdots \mid \mf{comp}\ \hat{e} \mid \mgrey{\vset{\ell}{p}}
\mid \mgrey{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}\\
e &::= \cdots \mid \mgrey{\hat{e}\ \blacktriangledown^\phi_p\ \hat{e}}\\
\text{Body } \hat{e} &::= e \mid \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}
\mid \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}\\
A &::= \cdots \mid A \arrow{E} A\\
\text{Effect } E &::= \mf{Pure} \mid \mf{Mut}
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\text{Phase } \phi &::= \mf{Init} \mid \mf{Succ}\\
\text{Meta-boolean flag } b &::= \mf{tt} \mid \mf{ff}\\
\text{State store } \sigma &::= \emptyset \mid \sigma, \ell \mapsto v\\
\text{Component memory } \pi &::= \{\mf{st}: \sigma, \mf{rerender}: b\}\\
\text{Program memory } m &::= \emptyset \mid m, p \mapsto \pi\\
\text{Label record } \Ell &::= \emptyset \mid \Ell, \ell: A\\
\text{Memory type } \Pi &::= \emptyset \mid \Pi, p: \Ell
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Type System}

We add $\Pi$ as a context to all typing rules (in the expected way).

We detail only the additional typing rules
and those which change significantly.

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lam] {
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e: A_2\ !\ E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \lambda x.e: A_1 \arrow{E} A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{comp}\ \hat{e}: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Set] {
  \ell: A \in \Pi[p]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \vset{\ell}{p}: A \arrow{\emut} \tone
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cell] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Pi[p]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, v): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A\ !\ E$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Val] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \mf{val}\ v: A\ !\ \mf{Pure}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: A_1\ !\ E
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A_1 \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: A_2\ !\ E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2}: A_2\ !\ E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-App] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1: A_1 \arrow{E} A_2
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_2: A_1
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v_1\ v_2: A_2\ !\ E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-If] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: \tbool
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_1: A\ !\ E
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e_2: A\ !\ E
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \eif{v}{e_1}{e_2}: A\ !\ E
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Fst] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A_1 \times A_2
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \efst{v}: A_1\ !\ \mf{Pure}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Snd] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A_1 \times A_2
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \esnd{v}: A_2\ !\ \mf{Pure}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Sub] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A\ !\ \mf{Pure}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A\ !\ \mf{Mut}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-EvalI] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Ell \cup \Pi[p]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \hat{e}_0\ \blacktriangledown^\mf{Init}_p\ \hat{e}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-EvalS] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}_0: \Pi[p]
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
  \and
  \Ell \subseteq \Pi[p]
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash
  \hat{e}_0\ \blacktriangledown^\mf{Succ}_p\ \hat{e}: \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TB-Lift] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \tview\ !\ \mf{Pure}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: \emptyset
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-Let] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash e: A\ !\ \mf{Pure}
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}: \Ell
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TB-State] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A
  \and
  \Gamma, x: A, x_\mf{set}: A \arrow{\emut} \tone 
  \semi \Pi \vdash \hat{e}: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash \estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}: \Ell, \ell: A
}
\end{mathpar}

We also define new judgments for memory typing:

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash \sigma: \Ell$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TS-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TS-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vdash v: A
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash \sigma: \Ell
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash \sigma, \ell \mapsto v: \Ell, \ell: A
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m: \Pi'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TM-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TM-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi_0 \vdash \sigma: \Ell
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Pi_0 \vDash m: \Pi
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Pi_0 \vDash
  m, p \mapsto \{\mf{st}: \sigma, \mf{rerender}: b\}: \Pi, p: \Ell
}
\end{mathpar}

(Abbreviate $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m: \Pi$ as $\Gamma \semi \Pi \vDash m$.)

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

We extend all step judgments with $m$ in the configuration,
and only reiterate those which alter $m$ in some way.

\fbox{$m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EE-Let] {
  m \semi e_1 \mapsto e_1' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2} \mapsto \elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2} \semi m'
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=EE-Eval] {
  m \semi \hat{e} \mapsto^\phi_p \hat{e}' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \hat{e}_0\ \blacktriangledown^\phi_p\ \hat{e}
  \mapsto
  \hat{e}_0\ \blacktriangledown^\phi_p\ \hat{e}' \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \vset{\ell}{p}\ v &\mapsto 
\mf{val}\ () \semi m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v
&&\textsc{EE-AppSet}\\
m \semi \hat{e}\ \blacktriangledown^\phi_p\ \mf{val}\ v
&\mapsto \elet{x}{\einit{v}}{\mf{val}\ \mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, x)} \semi m
&&\textsc{EE-EvalVal}\\
m \semi \einit{\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}}
&\mapsto
\hat{e}\ \blacktriangledown^\mf{Init}_p\ \hat{e}
\semi m, p \mapsto \{\mf{st}: \emptyset, \mf{rerender}: \ff\}
&&\textsc{EE-InitComp}\\
&\quad (p\ \text{fresh})\\
m \semi \esearch{id}{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
&\mapsto
\esearch{id}{u} \semi m
&&\textsc{ER-SrchCell}\\
m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
&\mapsto
\hat{e}\ \blacktriangledown^\mf{Succ}_p\ \hat{e} 
\semi m \mid m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff
&&\textsc{ER-ChkCT}\\
&\quad (\text{if}\ m[p].\mf{rerender} = \tt)\\
m \semi \echk{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, u)}
&\mapsto
\elet{x}{\echk{u}}{\mf{val}\ \mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, x)} \semi m
&&\textsc{ER-ChkCF}\\
&\quad (\text{if}\ m[p].\mf{rerender} = \ff)
\end{align*}

\fbox{$m \semi \hat{e} \mapsto^\phi_p \hat{e}' \semi m'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EB-Lift] {
  m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi e \mapsto^\phi_p e' \semi m'
}

\inferrule*[right=EB-Let] {
  m \semi e \mapsto e' \semi m'
} {
  m \semi \mf{let}\ x = e\ \mf{in}\ \hat{e}
  \mapsto^\phi_p
  \mf{let}\ x = e'\ \mf{in}\ \hat{e} \semi m'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
m \semi \mf{let}\ x=\mf{val}\ v\ \mf{in}\ \hat{e}
&\mapsto^\phi_p \hat{e}[v/x]
&&\textsc{EB-Val}\\
m \semi \mf{let}\ x, x_\mf{set} = \mf{useState}^\ell\ v\ \mf{in}\ \hat{e}
&\mapsto^\mf{Init}_p \hat{e}[v/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}] \semi m'
&&\textsc{EB-StateI}\\
&\quad (m' = m \mid m[p].\mf{st}[\ell] = v)\\
m \semi \mf{let}\ x, x_\mf{set} = \mf{useState}^\ell\ v\ \mf{in}\ \hat{e}
&\mapsto^\mf{Succ}_p \hat{e}[v'/x, \vset{\ell}{p}/x_\mf{set}]
\semi m
&&\textsc{EB-StateS}\\
&\quad (v' = m[p].\mf{st}[\ell])
\end{align*}

\subsection{$\lambda^\mf{ref}_\mf{dyn}$}

Extends $\lambda_\mf{dyn}$.

\subsubsection{Syntax}

As before, runtime-only constructs are greyed.
\begin{align*}
v &::= \cdots \mid \mf{comp}\ v \mid \mgrey{\ell} 
\mid \mgrey{\mf{cell}(v, v, u)}\\
e &::= \cdots \mid \eref{v} \mid \eget{v} \mid \eset{v}{v}\\
A &::= \cdots \mid \tref{A}\\
\sigma &::= \emptyset \mid \sigma, \ell \mapsto v\\
\Sigma &::= \emptyset \mid \Sigma, \ell: A
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Type System}

We add $\Sigma$ as a context to all typing rules (in the expected way).

We detail only the additional typing rules
and those which change significantly.

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: 
  \tone \rightarrow (
    (\tone \rightarrow \tview)
    \times \tref{\tbool} \times \tview)
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v \mf{comp}\ v: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \ell: A \in \Sigma
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v \ell: \tref{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cell] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_1: \tone \rightarrow \tview
  \quad
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_2: \tref{\tbool}
  \quad
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_3: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v \mf{cell}(v_1, v_2, v_3): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e e: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ref] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e \eref{v}: \tref{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Get] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: \tref{A}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e \eget{v}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_1: \tref{A}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e \eset{v_1}{v_2}: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

We extend all step judgments with $\sigma$ in the configuration,
and only reiterate those which alter $\sigma$ in some way.

\fbox{$\sigma \semi e \mapsto e' \semi \sigma'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=EE-Let] {
  \sigma \semi e_1 \mapsto e_1' \semi \sigma'
} {
  \sigma \semi \elet{x}{e_1}{e_2} \mapsto \elet{x}{e_1'}{e_2} \semi \sigma'
}
\end{mathpar}
\begin{align*}
\sigma \semi \eref{v} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \ell \semi \sigma, \ell \mapsto v
\quad (\ell\ \text{fresh})
&&\textsc{EE-Ref}\\
\sigma \semi \eget{\ell} &\mapsto \sigma[\ell] \semi \sigma,
&&\textsc{EE-Get}\\
\sigma \semi \eset{\ell}{v} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () \semi \sigma[\ell \mapsto v]
&&\textsc{EE-Set}\\
\sigma \semi \einit{\mf{comp}\ v}
&\mapsto
\elet{s, f, x}{v\ ()}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{t}{\einit{x}}{
    \mf{val}\ \mf{cell}(s,f,t)
  }
} \semi \sigma
&&\textsc{EE-InitComp}\\
\sigma \semi \esearch{id}{\mf{cell}(v_1, v_2, v_3)}
&\mapsto \esearch{id}{v_3} \semi \sigma
&&\textsc{EE-SrchCell}\\
\sigma \semi \echk{\mf{cell}(v_1,v_2,v_3)}
&\mapsto \elet{rr}{\eget{v_2}}{\\
  &\qquad \eif{rr\\
  &\qquad\ }{v_1\ ()\\
  &\qquad\ }{
    \elet{x}{\echk{v_3}\\
    &\qquad\quad}{\mf{cell}(v_1,v_2,x)}
  }
}
&&\textsc{EE-ChkCell}
\end{align*}

\subsection{$\lambda_{\mf{view}+}$}

Extends $\lambda_\mf{view}$.

\subsubsection{Syntax}

\begin{align*}
v &::= \cdots \mid \mf{rec}\ f(x).e \mid \mgrey{\mf{id}}
\mid \mgrey{\elit{v}} \mid \mgrey{\mf{node}(v, v, v)}\\
e &::= \cdots \mid v == v \mid \mgrey{\mf{fresh}}\\
&\quad\mid \ecase{v}{[]}{e}{x :: xs}{e}\\
&\quad\mid \mf{case}\ v\ \{
  \vattr{x}{x} \Rightarrow e \mid
  \vclick{x} \Rightarrow e
\}\\
&\quad\mid \mf{case}\ v\ \{
  \vtxt{x} \Rightarrow e \mid
  \vtag{x}{x} \Rightarrow e
\}\\
&\quad\mid \mf{case}\ v\ \{
  \elit{x} \Rightarrow e \mid
  \mf{node}(x, x, x) \Rightarrow e
\}\\
A &::= \cdots \mid \mf{ID}
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Type System}

\fbox{$\Gamma \vdash_v v: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Rec] {
  \Gamma, f: A_1 \rightarrow A_2, x: A_1 \vdash e: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \mf{rec}\ f(x).e: A_1 \rightarrow A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-ID] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \mf{id}: \mf{ID}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Lit] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tstr
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \elit{v}: \ttree
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Node] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: \mf{ID}
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \mf{List}\ \tattr
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_3: \mf{List}\ \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_v \mf{node}(v_1, v_2, v_3): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \vdash_e e: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Eq] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_1: \mf{ID}
  \and
  \Gamma \vdash_v v_2: \mf{ID}
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e v_1 == v_2: \tbool
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Fresh] { } {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \mf{fresh}: \mf{ID}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-CaseL] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \mf{List}\ A_1
  \quad
  \Gamma \vdash_e e_1: A_2
  \quad
  \Gamma, x: A_1, xs: \mf{List}\ A_1 \vdash_e e_2: A_2
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \ecase{v}{[]}{e_1}{x :: xs}{e_2}: A_2
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-CaseA] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tattr
  \quad
  \Gamma, x_1: \tstr, x_2: \tstr \vdash_e e_1: A
  \\
  \Gamma, x: \tone \rightarrow \tone \vdash_e e_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \ecase{v}{\vattr{x_1}{x_2}}{e_1}{\vclick{x}}{e_2}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-CaseV] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \tview
  \quad
  \Gamma, x: \tstr \vdash_e e_1: A
  \\
  \Gamma, x_1: \mf{List}\ \tattr, x_2: \mf{List}\ \tview \vdash_e e_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \ecase{v}{\vtxt{x}}{e_1}{\vtag{x_1}{x_2}}{e_2}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-CaseT] {
  \Gamma \vdash_v v: \ttree
  \quad
  \Gamma, x: \tstr \vdash_e e_1: A
  \\
  \Gamma, x_1: \mf{ID}, x_2: \mf{List}\ \tattr, x_3: \mf{List}\ \ttree 
  \vdash_e e_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \vdash_e \ecase{v}{\elit{x}}{e_1}{\mf{node}(x_1, x_2, x_3)}{e_2}: A
}
\end{mathpar}

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

\fbox{$e \mapsto e'$}
\begin{align*}
(\mf{rec}\ f(x).e)\ v &\mapsto e[v/x, (\mf{rec}\ f(x).e)/f]
&&\textsc{EE-AppR}\\
\mf{id} == \mf{id} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \mf{true} &&\textsc{EE-EqT}\\
\mf{id} == \mf{id}' &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \mf{false} \quad(\mf{id} \neq \mf{id}')
&&\textsc{EE-EqF}\\
\mf{fresh} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \mf{id} \quad(\mf{id}\ \mf{fresh})
&&\textsc{EE-Fresh}\\
\ecase{[]}{[]}{e_1}{x :: xs}{e_2} &\mapsto e_1
&&\textsc{EE-CaseNil}\\
\ecase{v :: vs}{[]}{e_1}{x :: xs}{e_2} &\mapsto e_2[v/x, vs/xs]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseCons}\\
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\ecase{\vattr{v_1}{v_2}&}{\vattr{x_1}{x_2}}{e_1}{\vclick{x}}{e_2}\\ 
&\mapsto e_1[v_1/x_1, v_2/x_2]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseAttr}\\
\ecase{\vclick{v}&}{\vattr{x_1}{x_2}}{e_1}{\vclick{x}}{e_2}\\ 
&\mapsto e_2[v/x]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseClick}\\
\ecase{\vtxt{v}&}{\vtxt{x}}{e_1}{\vtag{x_1}{x_2}}{e_2}\\ 
&\mapsto e_1[v/x]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseText}\\
\ecase{\vtag{v_1}{v_2}&}{\vtxt{x}}{e_1}{\vtag{x_1}{x_2}}{e_2}\\ 
&\mapsto e_2[v_1/x_1,v_2/x_2]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseTag}\\
\ecase{\elit{v}&}{\elit{x}}{e_1}{\enode{x_1}{x_2}{x_3}}{e_2}\\ 
&\mapsto e_1[v/x]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseLit}\\
\ecase{\enode{v_1}{v_2}{v_3}&}{\elit{x}}{e_1}{\enode{x_1}{x_2}{x_3}}{e_2}\\ 
&\mapsto e_2[v_1/x_1,v_2/x_2,v_3/x_3]
&&\textsc{EE-CaseNode}\\
\end{align*}

\subsection{$\lambda^\mf{ref}_{\mf{view}+}$}

Extends $\lambda_{\mf{view}+}$.

(We extend the destructors for $\tview$ and $\ttree$
with cases for $\mf{comp}$ and $\mf{cell}$ respectively;
these extensions are entirely standard and so are elided below.)

\subsubsection{Syntax}

\begin{align*}
v &::= \cdots \mid \mf{comp}\ v \mid \mgrey{\mf{cell}(v, v, v)} 
\mid \mgrey{\ell}\\
e &::= \cdots \mid \eref{v} \mid \eget{v} \mid \eset{v}{v}\\
A &::= \cdots \mid \tref{A}\\
\sigma &::= \emptyset \mid \sigma, \ell \mapsto v\\
\Sigma &::= \emptyset \mid \Sigma, \ell: A
\end{align*}

\subsubsection{Type System}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TV-Comp] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: 
  \tone \rightarrow (
    (\tone \rightarrow \tview)
    \times \tref{\tbool} \times \tview)
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v \mf{comp}\ v: \tview
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Loc] {
  \ell: A \in \Sigma
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v \ell: \tref{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TV-Cell] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_1: \tone \rightarrow \tview
  \quad
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_2: \tref{\tbool}
  \quad
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_3: \ttree
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v \mf{cell}(v_1, v_2, v_3): \ttree
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e e: A$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TE-Ref] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e \eref{v}: \tref{A}
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Get] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v: \tref{A}
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e \eget{v}: A
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TE-Set] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_1: \tref{A}
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_v v_2: A
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vdash_e \eset{v_1}{v_2}: \tone
}
\end{mathpar}

\fbox{$\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vDash \sigma: \Sigma'$}
\begin{mathpar}
\inferrule*[right=TS-Nil] { } {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma \vDash \emptyset: \emptyset
}
\and
\inferrule*[right=TS-Cons] {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma_0 \vdash v: A
  \and
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma_0 \vDash \sigma: \Sigma
} {
  \Gamma \semi \Sigma_0 \vDash \sigma, \ell \mapsto v: \Sigma, \ell: A
}
\end{mathpar}

Again abbreviate $\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vDash \sigma: \Sigma$
as $\Gamma \semi \Sigma \vDash \sigma$.

\subsubsection{Operational Semantics}

\fbox{$\sigma \semi e \mapsto e' \semi \sigma'$}
\begin{align*}
\sigma \semi \eref{v} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ \ell \semi \sigma, \ell \mapsto v
\quad (\ell\ \text{fresh})
&&\textsc{EE-Ref}\\
\sigma \semi \eget{\ell} &\mapsto \sigma[\ell] \semi \sigma,
&&\textsc{EE-Get}\\
\sigma \semi \eset{\ell}{v} &\mapsto \mf{val}\ () \semi \sigma[\ell \mapsto v]
&&\textsc{EE-Set}
\end{align*}

\section{Translations (WIP)}

\subsection{$\lambda^\mf{hook}_\mf{dyn}$ to $\lambda^\mf{ref}_\mf{dyn}$}

Types:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{A \arrow{E} A} &= \llrr{A} \rightarrow \llrr{A}\\
\end{align*}

Values:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\mf{comp}\ \hat{e}}_\I &= \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}}_\I\\
\llrr{\vset{\ell}{p}}_\I &= 
\lambda x.\elet{()}{\eset{\I(p,\ell)}{x}}{\eset{\I(p)}{\mf{true}}}\\
\llrr{\mf{cell}_p(\hat{e}, v)}_\I &= \mf{cell}(
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset},
  \I(p),
  \llrr{v}_\I
)
\end{align*}

Expressions:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{\hat{e}_0\ \blacktriangledown^{\mf{Init}}_p\ \hat{e}}_\I
&= \elet{s, f, v}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\emptyset}}{
  \elet{t}{\llrr{\einit{v}}}{\mf{val}\ \mf{cell}(s, f, t)}
}\\
\llrr{\hat{e}_0\ \blacktriangledown^{\mf{Succ}}_p\ \hat{e}}_\I
&= \elet{v}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset}}{
  \elet{t}{\einit{v}}{\mf{val}\ \mf{cell}(
    \lambda().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\emptyset},
    \I(p),
    t
  )}
}
\end{align*}

Bodies:
\begin{align*}
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta} &= \llrr{e}_\I\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}
&= \elet{x}{\eget{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad \eset{\delta_\I(p, \ell)}{y} 
        \semi 
        \eset{\delta_\I(p)}{\mf{true}}
      \\&\quad
    }}
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta}}
\end{align*}
\begin{align*}
\llrr{e}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}
= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{
  &\eret{(\lambda ().\llrr{\hat{e}_0}^{\mf{Succ}/p}_{\I/\delta},
  \delta_\I(p),x)}
}\\
\llrr{\elet{x}{e}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}
&= \elet{x}{\llrr{e}_\I}{\llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}}\\
\llrr{\estate{x}{\ell}{v}{\hat{e}}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta}
&= \elet{x_\ell}{\eref{\llrr{v}_\I}}{\\
  &\quad\ \elet{x}{\eget{x_\ell}}{\\
    &\quad\ \elet{x_\mf{set}}{\eret{\lambda y.\\
      &\qquad
      \eset{x_\ell}{y} \semi \eset{\delta(p)}{\mf{true}}}\\&\quad\ }{
       \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e}_0)/p}_{\I/\delta,\ell \mapsto x_\ell}
    }
  }
}\\
\llrr{\hat{e}}_\I &= \elet{x_f}{\eref{\mf{true}}}{
  \llrr{\hat{e}}^{\mf{Init}(\hat{e})/\emptyset}_{\I/\mf{flag} \mapsto x_f}
}
\end{align*}

$$\delta ::= \emptyset \mid \mf{flag} \mapsto x \mid \delta, \ell \mapsto x$$

\[\delta_\I(p, \ell) = \begin{cases}
  x & \text{if}\ \ell \mapsto x \in \delta\\
  \I(p, \ell) & \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\]
\[\delta_\I(p) = \begin{cases}
  x & \text{if}\ \mf{flag} \mapsto x \in \delta\\
  \I(p) & \text{otherwise}
\end{cases}\]

\subsection{$\lambda^\mf{ref}_\mf{dyn}$ to $\lambda^\mf{ref}_{\mf{view}+}$}

Constants (in $\lambda^\mf{ref}_{\mf{view}+}$,
employing some syntactic sugar):
\begin{align*}
\mf{map} &: (A_1 \rightarrow A_2) \rightarrow \mf{List}\ A_1 
\rightarrow \mf{List}\ A_2\\
\mf{map} &= \lambda f.\lambda xs.\ \mf{case}\ xs\ \{\\
&\quad\mid [] \Rightarrow []\\
&\quad\mid hd :: tl \Rightarrow f\ hd :: map\ f\ tl\\
\}\\
\mf{iter} &: (A \rightarrow \tone) \rightarrow \mf{List}\ A \rightarrow \tone\\
\mf{iter} &= \lambda f.\lambda xs.\ \mf{case}\ xs\ \{\\
&\quad\mid [] \Rightarrow ()\\
&\quad\mid hd :: tl \Rightarrow f\ hd \semi \mf{iter}\ f\ tl\\
\}\\
\mf{init} &: \tview \rightarrow \ttree\\
\mf{init} &= \lambda v.\ \mf{case}\ v\ \{\\
&\quad\mid \vtxt{x} \Rightarrow \elit{x}\\
&\quad\mid \vtag{x_1}{x_2} \Rightarrow 
  \mf{node}(\mf{fresh}, x_1, \mf{map}\ \mf{init}\ x_2)\\
&\quad\mid \mf{comp}\ x \Rightarrow \elet{s,f,v'}{x\ ()}{
  \mf{cell}(s,f,\mf{init}\ v')\\
}\}\\
\mf{check} &: \ttree \rightarrow \ttree\\
\mf{check} &= \lambda t.\ \mf{case}\ t\ \{\\
&\quad\mid \elit{x} \Rightarrow \elit{x}\\
&\quad\mid \mf{node}(x_1, x_2, x_3) \Rightarrow 
  \mf{node}(x_1, x_2, \mf{map}\ \mf{check}\ x_3)\\
&\quad\mid \mf{cell}(x_1, x_2, x_3) \Rightarrow 
  \eif{\eget{x_2}}{
    \mf{init}\ (x_1 ())
  } {
    \mf{cell}(x_1, x_2, \mf{check}\ x_3)
  }\\
\}\\
\mf{search} &: \mf{ID} \rightarrow \mf{Tree} \rightarrow \tone\\
\mf{search} &= \lambda id.\lambda t.\ \mf{case}\ t\ \{\\
&\quad\mid \elit{x} \Rightarrow ()\\
&\quad\mid \mf{node}(x_1, x_2, x_3) \Rightarrow 
  (\eif{x_1 == id}{\mf{scan}\ x_2}{()})
  \semi\\&\qquad\quad 
  \mf{iter}\ (\mf{search}\ id)\ x_3\\
&\quad\mid \mf{cell}(x_1, x_2, x_3) \Rightarrow \mf{search}\ id\ x_3\\
\}\\
\mf{scan} &: \mf{Attr} \rightarrow \tone\\
\mf{scan} &= \lambda a.\ \mf{case}\ a\ \{\\
  &\quad\mid \vattr{x_1}{x_2} \Rightarrow ()\\
  &\quad\mid \vclick{x} \Rightarrow x\ ()\\
\}
\end{align*}
